# [71/159] Theses/3110 - Nitto Boseki.md
**thesis · ticker: 3110 · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: Copper-Clad Laminate & PCB Materials · date: 2026-06-10 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

Nittobo is the single most-upstream chokepoint in the entire AI board-and-substrate materials stack: ~90% global share in low-CTE T-glass and ~60–70% in low-Dk NER-glass, yet it trades at a ~US$4.2B market cap, roughly 1/14th of its downstream CCL customer [[Theses/2383 - Elite Material]] (~US$57B) and a rounding error against the hyperscalers its glass cloth gates. Glass fabric is the warp-and-weft reinforcement woven into both the copper-clad laminate that becomes the AI server motherboard and the BT-resin core that becomes the GPU/ASIC package substrate; without glass cloth there is no PCB, no substrate, and no accelerator. The market still partly prices Nittobo as a cyclical Japanese glass-and-textiles industrial (the "Boseki" in the name literally means *spinning*), which is why a deeper monopoly than EMC or Ajinomoto carries a fraction of their valuation. The non-consensus tension is not whether the franchise is real, Goldman initiated Neutral acknowledging the 90% share, but whether a node-specific monopoly survives the two transitions arriving simultaneously: Asahi Kasei's quartz "Q-glass" contesting the M9 leading edge from H2 2026, and glass-core substrates threatening the T-glass franchise from 2030. At ~35x forward earnings after a +244% LTM run (and a −47% drawdown from the May-2026 peak in five weeks), the stock already pays for flawless execution of a capacity build that, by the company's own discipline, is deliberately slow.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if**: Nittobo NEZ/glass-fiber secures ≥50% of qualified M9 CCL design-wins through 2026–2027 (proving the moat re-compounds at the next node, not just the current one), AND Electronic Materials ex-one-off operating margin holds or expands through the first two quarters of new-capacity ramp.

→ **LOW if**: Asahi Kasei quartz (Q-glass) takes the majority of M9 leading-edge volume, OR electronic-glass-cloth ASP declines QoQ for two consecutive quarters in 2027 as new capacity (Nittobo + Nan Ya + competitors) qualifies into a softer demand window.

→ **CLOSE if**: glass-core substrates reach volume qualification at a flagship AI package AND Nittobo loses T-glass share at the leading edge with no offsetting low-Dk/PCB content gain: confirmation the highest-margin franchise is structurally displaced rather than cyclically soft.

# [73/159] Theses/6146 - DISCO.md
**thesis · ticker: 6146 · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: Semiconductor Capital Equipment · date: 2026-08-16 · metrics as of: 2026-08-16**

## Summary

DISCO is the closest thing semiconductors has to a true toll-booth monopoly that the market still prices as a cyclical: ~70–80% global share of wafer dicing, grinding and polishing, the mandatory back-end steps every die on Earth must pass through to be thinned and singulated, with a razor-and-blade consumables annuity (~30–35% of revenue) layered on an installed base no competitor can dislodge. The non-consensus tension is not whether DISCO is a good business (it is) but what you are actually buying: not the AI capex level (priced), but content-per-wafer compounding faster than the multiple de-rates. Every packaging architecture transition the vault already tracks independently (HBM stack height 12→16→24-Hi (each adds grind/dice passes), hybrid bonding (needs CMP-grade grinding), CoWoS→CoPoS panels (panel dicing), SiC/GaN via the Kabra laser) raises DISCO's dollar-content per wafer without it having to win a standard war or fend off a scaled rival (Accretech is ~¼ its size and a generation behind). The bear is the entry rather than the franchise. At ~48x trailing / ~34x forward earnings, on a stock whose 52-week range is ¥37,260–¥91,680, a ~60% peak-to-trough swing inside twelve months, the market has already demonstrated it treats DISCO as a high-beta AI-cycle instrument rather than a serene compounder, and there is no margin of safety at ~¥65,000. Medium conviction: a genuinely rare whole-industry monopoly with a consumables moat, diversified customers, ~70% gross margin and net cash, but bought here it is quality at a cyclical-premium price where the swing variable is hyperscaler-capex sentiment, not DISCO execution.

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ HIGH if:** consumables revenue holds or grows through a genuine equipment-order air-pocket (proving the annuity de-cyclicalizes the P&L) AND DISCO discloses rising content-per-wafer on HBM 16→24-Hi with no leading-edge share loss to Accretech/China — the semi-cyclical-compounder reclassification is confirmed and the multiple is earned; **OR** a valuation reset toward ~25–30x forward restores a margin of safety on the unchanged franchise.
- **→ LOW if:** consumables revenue falls *with* equipment in the next demand pocket (annuity is utilization-linked, not structural), OR HBM/AP content-intensity growth stalls for ≥2 quarters — DISCO re-rates back toward its cyclical 15–25x history.
- **→ CLOSE if:** a Chinese or Accretech vendor takes disclosed leading-edge grinding/dicing or hybrid-bonding-prep share from DISCO at a named customer, OR China self-sufficiency reaches leading-edge dice/grind parity — the monopoly commoditizes at the top.

# [74/159] Theses/6515 - WinWay Technology.md
**thesis · ticker: 6515 · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: Semiconductor Test Equipment · date: 2026-06-08 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

WinWay has become the world's #1 supplier of AI test sockets by owning the one part of chip test that gets harder every generation (the thermal problem), yet legacy market-share tables still file it as a small Taiwanese socket maker behind Yamaichi (28%) and Cohu (22%). The non-consensus core: a test socket is a **consumable** whose revenue-per-chip-tested rises with accelerator power, not unit volume. As AI GPUs/ASICs cross ~1kW TDP, the interface between the tester and the chip becomes a liquid-cooling engineering problem, and WinWay's world-first liquid-cooled test socket (US patent) plus HEATCon Titan thermal stack is a content/ASP step-up that the incumbents have not matched. Sub-7nm chips are 87% of its book, AI/HPC ~42-50% and rising, North America ~67-80% of revenue, and orders stretch 5-6 months out for the first time in its 25-year history. The business is genuinely high-quality (~44% gross margin, ~50% ROIC, net cash) but it trades at ~171x trailing / ~76x forward P/E after a +680% run, and ~67-80% of revenue rides on a handful of US AI-accelerator customers. The thesis is a quality-franchise-at-an-extreme-multiple call: own the structural AI test-interface leader, but the valuation and concentration mean the equity works on delivered earnings, not multiple expansion. Conviction medium, status draft.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if:** WinWay sustains >30% YoY revenue growth through 2027 with stable-to-rising gross margin AND discloses AI-socket or SLT share gains at a top-2 US AI accelerator (confirming durable design-in), AND the forward P/E de-rates below ~50x (valuation catches down to growth).

→ **LOW if:** gross margin compresses >300bp over 2 consecutive quarters (signal: competitor thermal parity / pricing pressure) OR Cohu/Yamaichi wins a liquid-cooling AI-socket design-in at Nvidia or AMD.

→ **CLOSE if:** AI/HPC revenue declines YoY for 2 consecutive quarters (signal: AI-capex digestion reaching the consumable layer) OR a top customer in-sources the test interface, removing >15% of revenue.

# [75/159] Theses/6857 - Advantest.md
**thesis · ticker: 6857.T · status: monitoring · conviction: medium · sector: Semiconductor Test Equipment · date: 2026-05-16 · metrics as of: 2026-08-15**

## Summary

Advantest is the pure-play winner of an under-modelled capacity step-function in HBM final test. HBM3 final test consumes ~6 hours per stack; HBM3e runs 8-10 hours; HBM4 will run 14-18 hours per credible vendor field-data, yet sell-side ATE capacity models still assume ~10 hours flat through HBM5. If 14-18 hours holds, the installed base of V93000 EXA Scale systems required to test 2027 HBM unit volumes is ~60-80% above current capacity. Advantest holds a dominant position in HBM final test (management discloses ~66% overall tester share, up ~10pp YoY, and a "majority" in AI accelerators; the ~95% HBM-final-test / ~80% high-end-logic figures are sell-side sub-segment estimates, not management-stated), has guided to scale V93000 SoC-tester production capacity from ~5,000 to ~10,000 systems per year (annual capacity, NOT installed base; the 10,000/yr level targeted end-CY2028 but "more likely into CY2029" per the Apr 2026 FY25 call), and is repositioning services revenue toward SEMICAP-style annuity (currently ~22% of revenue, targeted >30% by FY2027). The non-consensus angle is that the HBM test-time scaling curve is steeper than capacity adds, rather than "AI = test demand", creating a multi-year tester shortage that pricing already reflects partially but volumes have not yet caught. The bear setup is real: at ¥36,870 (¥26.7T, 45.5x FY2026e / 58x LTM) FY25 EPS ¥515 already cleared the May ¥420-480 book and the SEMICAP-premium multiple did not compress, so residual risk is de-rating, not a missed print; a single Teradyne win in HBM5 hybrid-bonding test qualification (plausible by Q4 2027) re-rates the position immediately. Medium conviction reflects the multiple, not the operating thesis.

## Conviction Triggers

```
→ HIGH if:
  - SK Hynix Q3 2026 earnings (Nov 2026) confirms HBM4 final test time >14 hours per stack in production with explicit per-stack disclosure, AND
  - Advantest Q3 FY2026 backlog disclosure (Jan-Feb 2027) shows V93000 system bookings >900 units for CY2027, AND
  - No Teradyne HBM qualification win at SK Hynix or Samsung disclosed through Q4 2027

→ LOW if:
  - Services revenue mix tracks below 22% of total at any Q1/Q3 FY2027 disclosure (signal: SEMICAP re-rating thesis failing), OR
  - Two consecutive quarters of V93000 system shipment guidance below 700 units/year cadence (signal: capacity step-function deferred or absent), OR
  - China entity-list expansion published by BIS that explicitly covers V93000 EXA Scale class testers (signal: ~$500M revenue gone, multiple compresses)

→ CLOSE if:
  - Teradyne wins at least one HBM5 hybrid-bonding memory qualification at Samsung or SK Hynix by Q4 2027 (signal: duopoly resumes, monopoly multiple invalidated), OR
  - Stock trades above ¥10,000 (~55-60x forward P/E on FY2027 consensus) — reflecting full SEMICAP re-rating priced in with no remaining margin of safety, OR
  - SK Hynix or Samsung publicly disclose parallel-test deployment (>16 channels per system) that compresses HBM4 effective test time per stack below 9 hours
```

# [76/159] Theses/6976 - Taiyo Yuden.md
**thesis · ticker: 6976 · status: draft · conviction: low · sector: MLCC & Power Semiconductors · date: 2026-08-14**

## Summary
The market has re-rated Taiyo Yuden to ~85x trailing / ~41x forward earnings as a "Japanese AI-MLCC winner" alongside Murata and Samsung Electro-Mechanics, but it is the smallest of the top three, earns a 23% gross margin (vs Murata's 42%), carries ~¥71B of net debt against Murata's ¥800B net cash, and generates 3.4% ROIC, below its cost of capital. The +91% FY2026 operating-profit jump and the 5 Aug 2026 guidance raise (FY2027 OP ¥30B → ¥45B, +50%) are substantially 2026-27 shortage rent (utilization, spot pricing 2-3x, FX), not a structural mix re-rate, because Taiyo Yuden is not a volume player at the 008004 sub-mm frontier where AI decoupling concentrates and where the durable qualification gate sits. The non-consensus read is bearish at this price: the market is paying a layer-monopoly multiple for a #4 follower whose genuine edge is a narrower niche (auto-grade high-temperature / high-voltage MLCC, where it is #1). The single unpriced call option that could flip that read: the 800VDC AI-rack transition pulls demand up the voltage and temperature axes, exactly Taiyo Yuden's niche, so its "auto-cyclical" specialty could become AI-central if it converts to disclosed server design-wins. Conviction low: the demand is real and the 800VDC option is genuine, but the risk/reward at ~85x on a substantially cyclical, sub-cost-of-capital, net-levered #4 is poor, and the base rate for a follower's shortage margin is give-back.

## Conviction Triggers
- **→ HIGH if**: Taiyo Yuden discloses a volume sub-mm (008004/01005) AI-accelerator design-win at a Western hyperscaler at Murata/SEMCO's expense, AND operating margin holds ≥13% through a book-to-bill <1.05 quarter, proving structural frontier entry plus durable margin rather than shortage rent.
- **→ LOW if**: MLCC book-to-bill falls below 1.0 for two consecutive quarters with spot premiums compressing >30% and OPM reverting below ~8%; the shortage rent is giving back and the thesis is confirmed cyclical.
- **→ CLOSE if**: consensus FY28 OP is revised below ¥30B (the pre-raise base) on capacity-glut normalization, OR forward P/E stays >45x while ROIC remains <7%; i.e. paying a structural multiple for sub-cost-of-capital returns with no frontier moat.

Consensus models Murata as a saturated passive-components supplier with growth pegged to smartphone units and a slow erosion in commodity MLCC share to Chinese suppliers. The live question is whether mid-60s trailing is still too much to pay for a durability that must compound for years to justify the multiple.

## Conviction Triggers
<!-- Pre-defined, falsifiable if/then statements. Review each time you update the thesis. -->

# [78/159] Theses/AAOI - Applied Optoelectronics.md
**thesis · ticker: AAOI · status: draft · conviction: low · sector: Optical Networking & Photonics · date: 2026-05-24 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

Applied Optoelectronics is the only US-listed merchant pluggable optical transceiver vendor that operates its own indium phosphide fab: a vertically integrated profile that superficially resembles [[Theses/LITE - Lumentum|Lumentum]] but with one decisive difference: AAOI's InP fab makes 1310nm DFB lasers for CATV and lower-speed datacom, not the 200G EMLs that gate the 1.6T transition. The company is therefore a hybrid: half cable-broadband infrastructure incumbent (CATV segment, $320M FY26 run-rate, Charter + Mediacom 1.8GHz amplifier certifications), half 800G/1.6T transceiver assembler dependent on merchant EMLs from Lumentum / Broadcom / Mitsubishi. The 2026 narrative (FY26 revenue guide raised to >$1.1B (+128% YoY) on $200M+ 1.6T order from a hyperscaler, $124M+ 800G orders, Microsoft + Amazon ($4B/10yr) + Oracle as primary datacenter customers, plus 700K-unit/month combined 800G+1.6T capacity by end-2027) has driven a ~915% 1-year rally that has since retraced to a ~$10B market cap (~$120/share, off the $173 peak), still an extreme multiple on a -22% NTM ROIC and negative FCF, but no longer the $14-18B/175x-forward top-tick the thesis was written against. The non-consensus angle is that AAOI is a structurally lower-moat path into the optics supercycle than its multiple implies: it does not own the 200G EML chokepoint that determines who wins 1.6T (Lumentum does), it does not own the silicon photonic integration stack (Broadcom / TSMC do), it does not own the DSP layer (Marvell / Broadcom do), and the LPO win with Microsoft is a customer-specific architecture choice subject to Microsoft's commercial leverage. Culper Research's 2017+ short thesis (refreshed periodically) alleges Microsoft retains IP and exclusivity rights, using AAOI as a bargaining chip against Coherent / Innolight pricing. Position is sized as a small convex bet (0.5–1% of semi NAV per the 2026-05-24 portfolio rebalance), not a structural position; conviction low pending evidence that gross margin scales past 30% on the 1.6T mix shift and that customer concentration de-risks via the Amazon $4B agreement landing as committed volume rather than ceiling-allocation.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if:**
- Q3 2026 or Q4 2026 gross margin prints ≥33% on quarterly revenue ≥$250M (validates structural margin expansion AND scale)
- A second hyperscaler (AWS or Meta) discloses LPO program with AAOI at ≥$200M annualized run-rate (refutes Culper IP-exclusivity allegation AND breaks Microsoft concentration)
- AAOI announces a 6-inch InP roadmap or 200G EML internal-production path with credible 2027-2028 milestone (closes the structural BOM disadvantage vs Lumentum / Coherent)

→ **LOW if:**
- Q2 2026 OR Q3 2026 gross margin prints below 28% (structural margin compression confirmed; bull case requires 32-35% by end-2027)
- 1.6T module ASPs drop below $1,000 industry-wide by end-2026 (faster ASP collapse than 800G analog implies)
- CPO captures ≥20% of NVIDIA + Broadcom hyperscale switch shipments in H2 2026 (faster disintermediation timeline than base case)
- New equity issuance announced before Q4 2026 in excess of $300M (signals capacity buildout requires more capital than CATV + datacenter cash flow can support)
- Microsoft renegotiates pricing or shifts ≥20% of LPO volume to Cloud Light / Coherent in 2026 reporting periods

→ **CLOSE if:**
- Microsoft formally winds down or materially reduces LPO program with AAOI at any point through 2027 (eliminates the architectural differentiator)
- AAOI misses FY26 revenue guide by ≥15% ($1.1B → <$935M) on a non-macro driver (execution failure, not industry-wide capex pause)
- Culper Research or equivalent short report uncovers material new evidence of revenue recognition / customer accounting irregularity
- Broadcom OR NVIDIA discloses pluggable transceiver demand from hyperscalers stepping down in 2027-2028 capex plans (structural CPO acceleration)
- Stock re-rates to >$280 (~50% above current $173) without commensurate margin expansion or customer diversification (sell into terminal-velocity narrative)

# [79/159] Theses/AEHR - Aehr Test Systems.md
**thesis · ticker: AEHR · status: active · conviction: medium · sector: Semiconductor Capital Equipment · date: 2026-04-29 · metrics as of: 2026-08-16**

## Summary

AEHR is mispriced as a SiC-burn-in pure play in the middle of a forced re-classification into a multi-end-market wafer-level test platform. Q3 FY2026 (April 2026) crystallized the transition: revenue collapsed 43.7% YoY to $10.3M as the SiC cycle drained the legacy backlog, but bookings hit $37.2M (5x QoQ, book-to-bill >3.5x) on a then-record $50.9M effective backlog, with AI processor customers then >35% of the book and a hyperscale silicon photonics design-in landed in March 2026. FY26 printed $50.0M; FY27 is guided $130-150M (2.6-3.0x) on $100.6M effective backlog. Sell-side already discounts "SiC recovers". The non-consensus claim is that AEHR's FOX-XP installed base, optimized for high-power (3,500W/wafer) and high-parallelism (9x300mm) burn-in, is the only commercially deployed wafer-level burn-in (WLBI) platform that scales to AI accelerator and CPO test bottlenecks before Advantest/Teradyne build native WLBI capacity. The platform is non-fungible inside that window. Conviction is medium: the inflection is real and the $130-150M guide is signed against backlog, but FY26 10-K still puts 70% of revenue in the top five (largest 26%), the stock is ~85x EV/sales on printed $50.0M and ~28-33x on that FY27 guide, and equipment lumpiness precludes high conviction until at least two more concentrated wins disperse the mix.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if**: (a) Q4 FY26 (June-July 2026) discloses a second AI customer at >$5M order size AND (b) gross margin recovers to >42% in Q4 FY26 AND (c) FY27 guide implied at $80M+ revenue. All three conditions signal platform-not-customer, margin durability, and demand visibility.

→ **HIGH if**: SiPh customer signs a $10M+ systems order before end of CY2026 — would validate optical WLBI thesis a year earlier than base case.

→ **LOW if**: Q4 FY26 customer concentration remains >75% AND no new design-in is disclosed AND book-to-bill falls below 1.5x. Signals the AI inflection was a single-customer event, not a platform breakout.

→ **LOW if**: Advantest or Teradyne announces a WLBI-capable platform partnership or roadmap (specifically: native multi-wafer high-power burn-in capability) at SEMICON Japan 2026 or in the Teradyne FY27 product roadmap call.

→ **CLOSE if**: Lead AI customer is disclosed (in 10-K or earnings) as a single hyperscaler captive (Google TPU, AWS Trainium) AND that captive's annual unit volume guidance falls below 100K units — destroys the volume scaling assumption.

→ **CLOSE if**: Q1 FY27 (Sep 2026) bookings come in below $15M AND backlog shrinks below $40M — implies the FY26 booking surge was front-loading, not a sustained inflection.

**thesis · ticker: AIXA · status: active · conviction: medium · sector: Semiconductor Capital Equipment · date: 2026-04-29 · metrics as of: 2026-08-16**

## Summary

Aixtron is being repriced from a cyclical SiC/GaN power-equipment supplier into the dominant MOCVD toolmaker for the InP photonics buildout, but the market has not yet underwritten how complete the application mix has flipped: optoelectronics moved from 12% of equipment revenue in 2024 to 23% in 2025, and Q1 2026 order intake hit 65% optoelectronics, a structural transition compressed into four quarters. The thesis is that AIXA captures a near-monopolistic share of a multi-year InP capacity wave (Lumentum Greensboro, Coherent, Nokia G10-AsP, SMART Photonics, plus three undisclosed leading laser makers) at the same time GaN-on-Si data-center power demand layers on as an independent second leg via NVIDIA's 800V transition expected in 2027. Despite a +138% YTD rally to €41 (~€4.7B) and Q1 2026's -38% EBIT operating deleverage (revenue €59.4M), NTM P/E is ~55x on trough FY2026E €0.74 EPS: the mix-shift is in the NTM multiple. On FY2027E €1.34 consensus the stock is still ~31x, inside the SiC-equipment 30-40x band, vs LITE/COHR at 48-60x: the gap is now a 2027-earnings claim, not an NTM one. The market still prices AIXA as a SiC equipment cycle name with a photonics call option rather than a photonics enabler with a SiC tail. Initial conviction medium pending Apr 30 Q1 2026 results validating mix-shift durability, FY guide reaffirmation, and free cash flow trajectory.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if**: Q1 2026 earnings (Apr 30) confirms ≥60% optoelectronics order share, FY2026 €560M guide is reaffirmed or raised, AND Q3 2026 results disclose ≥40% optoelectronics share of equipment revenue with EBIT margin tracking ≥20%. The combination validates the mix-shift thesis as durable, not a one-quarter spike, with operating leverage materializing as the revenue base normalizes.

→ **LOW if**: Q1 2026 earnings disclose Q2 backlog conversion below 80% of normal seasonal pace, OR FY2026 guide is cut by ≥5%, OR Veeco announces a 6-inch InP MOCVD product with named first customer at any 2026 event (CS Mantech May, SEMICON West July, Veeco earnings). Any of these signals the thesis economics are eroding faster than base case.

→ **CLOSE if**: FY2026 actual results (Feb 2027) print optoelectronics share <30% of equipment revenue AND FY2027 guide is below current €690M consensus by ≥10%, OR Veeco ships competitive 6-inch InP MOCVD with multiple confirmed customers by Q3 2027. Either signals the structural thesis (InP monopoly + accelerating mix shift) has failed to materialize, with no second-leg story strong enough to compensate.

# [81/159] Theses/ALAB - Astera Labs.md
**thesis · ticker: ALAB · status: draft · conviction: low · sector: Custom Silicon & Networking Semiconductors · date: 2026-08-16 · metrics as of: 2026-08-16**

## Summary

Astera Labs is mid-flip from a PCIe/CXL retimer analog into a second merchant switching P&L below Broadcom, and the market has already partly repriced it as such. Q2 FY2026 (Aug 4) printed $392.4M revenue (+104% YoY, +27% sequential), non-GAAP EPS $0.80 (eighth straight beat), with PCIe 6 products crossing 50% of revenue and management guiding Scorpio to become the largest product family in Q3, a quarter ahead of plan. The mechanism is a dollar-content step-function: content per XPU moves from $50–100 at IPO toward "multiple $1,000s," the 320-lane Scorpio X scale-up switch alone worth ~$1,000/XPU. The category flip is real; the non-consensus tension is that at ~150x forward earnings / ~21x forward sales the switch-company outcome is priced while the two facts that would break it are not: one customer was >70% of 2025 revenue (the Feb-2026 Amazon $6.5B warrant deepens the dependence, not diversifies it), and the vault's own SAN-for-DRAM analog assigns Astera the QLogic/Emulex role, a layer that commoditized within ~5 years of the build-out peak as Broadcom, Marvell, and Microchip crowd both the retimer and the switch. Low conviction reflects a high-quality franchise at a price with no margin of safety and a binary anchor: a name to own on a valuation reset plus qual-conversion + customer-diversification proof, not at 150x into a single socket.

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ HIGH if:** Scorpio is the largest product family for ≥2 consecutive quarters, AND ≥3 of the 10 pre-production customers convert to disclosed/named production revenue including ≥1 non-AWS scale-up socket (neocloud or add-in-card GPU vendor), AND top-customer concentration is disclosed <55% of revenue — the reclassification to a multi-customer switch compounder is confirmed with a de-risked anchor.
- **→ LOW if:** top customer remains >70% of revenue through FY2026 AND fewer than 2 of the 10 quals convert to production by Q2 FY2027 — the switch-company story stays a single-anchor Trainium SKU.
- **→ CLOSE if:** Amazon materially in-sources scale-up connectivity or shifts Scorpio-class content to Broadcom/Marvell on a Trainium generation, OR Broadcom Atlas / Marvell take a disclosed Scorpio socket — the merchant scale-up layer commoditizes at the anchor.

**thesis · ticker: AMAT · status: active · conviction: high · sector: Semiconductor Capital Equipment · date: 2026-04-29 · metrics as of: 2026-08-15**

## Summary

Applied Materials is the broadest single-vendor exposure to four simultaneous architectural inflections, GAA transistors, backside power delivery (BSPDN), HBM/advanced packaging, and ICAPS recovery, but the April "valuation play vs LRCX" frame is spent: at $507 (~$403B, ~41x NTM / ~30x FY27E) AMAT no longer screens as the cheap WFE prime after the $397→$570→$507 path and the FY27E reset ($11.39→$17.05). The market still bundles it with the WFE cohort whose narrative is dominated by China export drag and ICAPS softness. The actual asymmetry sits in (1) tool revenue per wafer step expanding 1.5–2x at 2nm vs. 5nm because GAA + BSPDN multiply deposition/etch/CMP intensity per layer, (2) a $1.5B → $3B HBM bookings ramp that is currently obscured by the dominant logic/foundry mix, (3) a co-developed Kinex hybrid bonder with BESI (AMAT owns 9% of BESI) that converts a packaging tailwind into an equity option, and (4) the EPIC Center opening Spring 2026 as a 3+ year customer-lock-in flywheel that is invisible in current consensus estimates. The bear case is real: ASML overtook AMAT as #1 WFE vendor in 2024, ASMI has a structural ALD lead heading into 2nm, and AMEC/NAURA China substitution erodes ICAPS, which is why conviction is medium rather than high.

## Conviction Triggers

**→ HIGH if**: AMAT confirms ALD step share gain at TSMC N2 (vs. ASMI) on the next 2 earnings calls AND HBM revenue trajectory crosses $2B annualized run-rate by Q3 FY26 AND ICAPS bookings show sequential acceleration in 2 of next 3 quarters AND first EPIC Center co-located customer partnership announced with TSMC, Intel, OR Samsung at "embedded process integration team" scope. Three of four required for HIGH conviction.

**→ LOW if**: ASMI discloses >55% ALD share at 2nm at next earnings AND/OR AMAT WFE share data (Gartner) shows further decline below 18% in 2026 AND/OR China revenue exits FY26 at <20% mix with explicit AMEC tool-of-record displacement at SMIC. Any one of three triggers downgrades to LOW.

**→ CLOSE if**: Two simultaneous failures — ALD lost AND ICAPS extends trough through FY27 — with sell-side consensus EPS revised down >15% from current $11.39 FY27. Position becomes a value trap rather than growth-at-reasonable-price; close and rotate to higher-conviction WFE name (LRCX, ASML, or BESI).

## Summary

AMD is the sole merchant full-stack alternative to Nvidia at a moment when every hyperscaler has concluded it cannot afford single-source GPU dependence. The OpenAI 6GW deal and Meta 6GW deal signed inside 5 months were engineered by customers who needed a duopoly to restore pricing leverage against Nvidia, not won on hardware merit. AMD traded 10% of its equity (OpenAI warrant at $0.01 over 160M shares) to lock itself in as the structural second source, converting a GPU supply contract into a decade-long capital alignment. At ~$839B market cap and ~68x forward P/E the stock has pulled back from the July ~$910B / 75x print that sat in its own 2028 bull-case zone two years early, but 68x is still flawless-execution pricing: continued EPYC share capture (41% Q2 2025 tracking toward parity with Intel), and ROCm reaching effective CUDA parity by H2 2026, each individually plausible, the combination tight. The non-consensus case is that the hyperscaler-initiated diversification mandate is structurally mispriced as a cyclical market-share trade when it is actually a durable rewiring of AI infrastructure procurement. The bear case is that the OpenAI warrant is dilution, the MI400 is one generation behind Rubin, the ROCm asymptote is farther than disclosed, and the valuation embeds a second-source premium that vanishes the moment Nvidia cuts prices.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if**: MLPerf Training v5.0 shows MI455X within 10% of Rubin GR200 on Llama 5-class training workloads, **AND** Meta publicly confirms Llama 5 production-trained on ROCm, **AND** a 3rd hyperscaler (Google, AWS) signs a commitment ≥2GW by end Q3 2026. (Any 2 of 3 qualifies as conviction strengthened; all 3 triggers HIGH.)

→ **LOW if**: MI450/Helios ships with a benchmark gap >25% to Rubin GR200 on published MLPerf results, **OR** OpenAI/Meta renegotiate commitments downward by >30% on realized take-or-pay, **OR** CoWoS 2027 allocation disclosed at <12% (no structural improvement from 2026 bottleneck).

→ **CLOSE if**: AI revenue in FY2027 prints below $20B (vs. "tens of billions" guidance implying $25–40B+), **OR** ROCm is publicly de-prioritized by any top-3 hyperscaler in favor of CUDA-only or ASIC-only workloads, **OR** Intel 18A Clearwater Forest reclaims server CPU share trajectory (AMD EPYC share falls below 35% in any quarter through 2027).

# [85/159] Theses/ARM - Arm Holdings.md
**thesis · ticker: ARM · status: draft · conviction: low · sector: Compute & AI Compute Accelerators · date: 2026-06-09 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

The market is celebrating ARM crossing ~50% hyperscaler CPU share and launching its first in-house chip (the AGI CPU, codenamed Phoenix) as a clean datacenter-AI growth story, tripling the stock to ~$412 (+277% YTD) at ~200x forward EPS, ~80x EV/sales, and ~38% above the $245 mean analyst target. This re-rating conflates **ISA share with royalty capture** and prices the vertical-integration pivot as pure upside while ignoring that ARM is funding it by spending the one asset that made the IP model work: ecosystem neutrality. ARM's highest-value datacenter customers (NVIDIA's custom Olympus core, Qualcomm's Nuvia-derived Oryon, Apple, and increasingly Google/Amazon in-house silicon) are designing away from stock Neoverse onto Architecture Licenses that pay lower per-chip royalties, so the 50%-share headline overstates dollar capture per high-end socket. The AGI CPU competes head-on with the same Graviton/Axion/Cobalt/Vera licensees that drive the share; the FTC opened a formal antitrust probe (May 15, 2026) into whether ARM is degrading those licenses; and ARM lost the Qualcomm/Nuvia case meant to force higher royalties. Conviction is **low**: the franchise is elite (98.3% gross margin, 22M+ developers, 350B+ cumulative chips, third straight year of 20%+ revenue growth) and the rate×content royalty engine (Armv9 ~5% vs ~2.5–3% for v8; CSS >10%) is genuinely durable, but at ~200x forward earnings with operating margin already declining (43.0% from 46.7%) and $882M FCF (~0.2% yield), the price embeds flawless multi-year execution of a strategy that is simultaneously antagonizing the ecosystem and inviting RISC-V defection. Negative skew at this entry; a great franchise at a broken price.

## Conviction Triggers

**→ HIGH if**: blended datacenter royalty-per-chip rises for ≥2 consecutive quarters **AND** the AGI CPU converts its >$2B FY27–28 order book into shipping revenue with ≥2 *named hyperscaler endorsements* beyond Meta (proving vertical integration is additive, not ecosystem-destructive) **AND** the stock has de-rated below ~80x forward. (All three: strategy works + sane price.)

**→ LOW if**: ARM trades >150x forward and >30% above the mean analyst target while operating margin continues to decline **AND** a top-5 datacenter licensee publicly commits a flagship server program to RISC-V or fully custom non-ARM ISA. (This is approximately the current state; the trigger formalizes what keeps conviction low.)

**→ CLOSE if**: the FTC issues a formal complaint or consent decree materially constraining ARM's license terms **OR** two of {Qualcomm, NVIDIA, a top-3 hyperscaler} publicly shift a flagship CPU roadmap off ARM to RISC-V: either event breaks the royalty-base durability that underwrites any valuation.

# [86/159] Theses/ASMI - ASM International.md
**thesis · ticker: ASMI · status: active · conviction: high · sector: Semiconductor Capital Equipment · date: 2026-04-29 · metrics as of: 2026-08-15**

## Summary

ASMI is mispriced as an "ALD pure-play with cyclical capex exposure" when it is structurally a process-of-record (POR) annuity business with two compounding engines (ALD + Epi) and ~$400-500M SAM expansion per logic node. The sell-side framing of "55% ALD market share" obscures the real economics: each node win locks ASMI into 5-7 years of consumables-like equipment revenue across every wafer the foundry runs at that geometry, and the 1.4nm transition (TSMC pilot H2 2026, Samsung 2027) is doubling ALD intensity per wafer through gate-all-around (GAA), backside power delivery, and dipole engineering layers. The market is pricing ASMI on ~41x LTM / ~39x NTM after 2026E EPS was marked €16→€23.1: multiple compressed on an earnings upgrade, not a POR downgrade. Halo/Trillium still attack adjacencies (Mo ALD for NAND wordlines, batch ALD for spacers) rather than the HKMG stack core; N3/N2 POR annuity funds the next decade regardless of that noise. With CFET (epi-grown stacked channels) emerging as the post-GAA architecture beyond 2030, ASMI's epi franchise (currently treated as a #2 footnote behind AMAT) becomes a second compounding leg. Conviction medium pending verification of 1.4nm POR slot count.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if** ASMI confirms 1.4nm POR slot count is incremental (not just maintained) at both TSMC and Samsung, with management quantifying SAM expansion ≥$500M per 100K WSPM (vs $400M at 2nm) by Q4 2026 earnings.

→ **HIGH if** ASMPT stake is divested at ≥$1.2B with proceeds committed to buyback, AND Q3 2026 ALD bookings exceed consensus by ≥10%.

→ **LOW if** Lam ALTUS Halo or AMAT Trillium wins POR at TSMC 1.4nm for any HKMG-critical layer (work-function metal, dipole, or capping), confirmed by tool-shipment commentary or industry channel checks.

→ **LOW if** consolidated 2027 hyperscaler capex guidance (Microsoft + Meta + Google + Amazon) declines >10% YoY in Q4 2026 / Q1 2027 prints.

→ **CLOSE if** combined hyperscaler capex cuts >25% sustained over 2 consecutive quarters AND ASMI multiple has not compressed to <20x (i.e., the market has not priced the cycle and the position is exposed to multiple compression).

→ **CLOSE if** MATCH Act final rule includes retroactive servicing ban AND ASMI installed-base China impairment exceeds 8% of revenue.

# [88/159] Theses/BE - Bloom Energy.md
**thesis · ticker: BE · status: draft · conviction: low · sector: Data Center Power & Cooling · date: 2026-07-29**

## Summary

Bloom Energy is the only scaled commercial solid-oxide fuel-cell (SOFC) platform, and the market prices it (~$50B, ~12x forward / ~16x trailing sales, ~55x book) as a durable winner-take-most toll on AI-datacenter power. The non-consensus read is that Bloom sells a **time-to-power arbitrage rather than a layer monopoly**: its decisive edge is 50–90-day deployment while gas turbines carry 5–7-year backlogs and the grid does not materially improve until ~2035. That arbitrage is real and lucrative today (Q2 2026 revenue +166% to $1.07B, non-GAAP gross margin 34%, FY26 guide $3.9–4.2B), but it is a wasting asset: GE combined-cycle turbines match Bloom's ~54% efficiency at ~1/100th the unit count and lower fueled cost, and turbine/SMR/grid supply all normalize into 2028–2030, the same window the [[Website/2026-07-29 - Sustainability of AI Capex|AI-capex digestion]] is most likely to reprice financed AI capacity. The thesis lives or dies on backlog quality: Bloom cites a $20B "contracted backlog" against just **$492.6M of audited remaining performance obligations** (a >40x gap a short-seller has attacked; the AEP 1GW order has already slipped from 2028 to "no later than 2030"). We own the real bottleneck; we distrust the valuation and the backlog until audited numbers and hyperscale-scale (Tier-IV, primary-power) proof catch up. Low conviction, draft: a watch-for-digestion-trough entry rather than a frenzy-phase buy.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if:** audited RPO steps up to **>$3B** AND Bloom signs ≥1 *repeat* hyperscale primary-power order at Tier-IV scale while turbine backlogs remain **>4 years** — the arbitrage is converting to a durable annuity before the bottleneck clears.
→ **LOW if:** audited RPO stays **<$1B through FY2026** OR any flagship order (AEP/Oracle) slips again or cancels OR order intake decelerates as turbine lead times shorten. *(Thesis is initiated at low; this is the confirm-the-bear trigger toward CLOSE.)*
→ **CLOSE if:** the short-seller backlog/China allegations are substantiated (RPO restatement, ITC disqualification, or SEC action) OR two consecutive quarters of order-intake decline with turbine lead times normalizing — the arbitrage is over and the backlog was soft.

- **→ LOW if:** power-battery GM compresses below ~18% for 2+ quarters (commoditization winning) **OR** global EV-battery share falls below 35% **OR** China EV-battery volume declines YoY for 2+ consecutive quarters.
- **→ CLOSE if:** a US/allied action forces unwind of the LRS model (Ford/GM licensing killed) **AND** a China-side event (entity-list escalation, forced tech transfer, or H-share capital controls) simultaneously impairs the equity: both the growth workaround and the ownership vehicle breaking together.

# [93/159] Theses/CBRS - Cerebras Systems.md
**thesis · ticker: CBRS · status: draft · conviction: low · sector: Compute & AI Compute Accelerators · date: 2026-07-09 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

Cerebras is a leveraged, binary bet on one proposition: that the reasoning/agentic era shifts AI value from training FLOPS to decode speed, and that its wafer-scale, SRAM-only architecture is the structurally correct, and, after Nvidia absorbed Groq, the last independent, way to win the fast-inference tier. This is not an "Nvidia-killer" thesis, and Cerebras is not one. The non-consensus angle is that the market lumps CBRS with the dead-challenger pile (Graphcore/Nervana/Habana/SambaNova) and caps it at "<1% share forever," while the competitive field just thinned and the architecture's core liability (SRAM capital inefficiency) is precisely what makes it the latency leader where value is migrating. Against that sits a genuinely ugly cap table of risks: 86% of 2025 revenue from two Abu Dhabi entities, a $24.6B backlog that is ~85% a single customer (OpenAI) backend-loaded to 2028+, no ecosystem moat, cash losses, a 75x R&D disadvantage to Nvidia, and ~100x trailing sales into a mid-November 2026 lockup cliff. This is a barbell-convex, venture-stage position in public-market clothing, sized small or not at all; it is not a compounder. SuperNova / CS-4 (August 2026) is a same-silicon clock-and-rack refresh that can double token revenue at similar hardware spend while leaving the 44GB SRAM-capacity wall intact — a manufacturability print, not a WSE-4 capital-efficiency print. Conviction starts **low**.

## Conviction Triggers

```
→ HIGH if: cumulative OpenAI-recognized revenue ≥ ~$300M by FY2027 AND UAE
  (MBZUAI + G42) concentration falls below 50% of revenue AND cloud gross margin
  holds above 35% for two consecutive quarters.

→ LOW if: OpenAI revenue recognition slips two consecutive quarters vs. the
  15%/24-month schedule, OR a restatement results from the disclosed internal-
  control weaknesses, OR Nvidia Rubin + Groq LPU demonstrates decode within ~3x
  of CS-3 on mainstream open models.

→ CLOSE if: a U.S. export-control action materially restricts MBZUAI/G42
  shipments, OR OpenAI cancels/renegotiates the 750MW commitment downward, OR
  the investment case reduces to a single-customer sovereign-hardware vendor
  (UAE > 70% of revenue with no cloud diversification by end-2027).
```

Conviction starts **low**: pre-chasm, binary-outcome, single-anchor, no identified durable moat, ~100x trailing sales. The triggers above are the falsifiable observables to re-rate against.

# [95/159] Theses/COHR - Coherent.md
**thesis · ticker: COHR · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: Optical Networking & Photonics · date: 2026-08-16 · metrics as of: 2026-08-16**

## Summary

Coherent is the vertically integrated #2 of the AI-optics supercycle, the only company that owns every layer from 6-inch indium phosphide substrate through EML/CW/VCSEL lasers, silicon-photonic PICs, pluggable transceivers, optical circuit switches, and coherent transport, and the market is mispricing that breadth as conglomerate drag rather than as the widest single-vendor capture of the copper-to-optics conversion. FY26 revenue reached $7.12B (+22.5%), Datacenter & Communications hit 79% of the mix growing 58.6% YoY, FY27 is "basically completely booked out" with customers already placing POs into calendar 2028, and NVIDIA's $2B common-equity investment (March 2026) locks multi-year capacity and access. Yet the stock trades ~24% below its June ATH at ~44x forward, a discount to pure-play Lumentum's ~50x, because the core bull, a world-first 6-inch InP platform at 4× device density and ~60% lower cost per laser, has not yet reached gross margin: COHR printed 40.2% non-GAAP GM against Lumentum's 50.4%, and a credible engineering bear reads that ~10-point gap as evidence the 6-inch advantage is real on wafer cost but not on the parametric spec (RIN, PCE, wavelength grid) the highest-margin CPO lasers require. The investable question is not whether AI optics grows (it does) but whether COHR's 6-inch ramp closes the margin gap to Lumentum through FY27, converting a booked order book and a −$1B free-cash-flow capex bet into the >42% gross margin and >$3B/quarter the company guides to. Medium conviction: a real, deleveraged (0.7×), NVIDIA-backed multi-layer platform at a reasonable multiple, held back from high by an unresolved yield-quality question, deeply negative near-term FCF, and a structurally second-place position on the one layer (InP lasers) that actually prices the cycle.

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ HIGH if**: COHR non-GAAP GM crosses ~44% by FQ4'27 (toward the 42%+ target) with the datacenter GM gap to Lumentum narrowing to <5 points, AND operating cash flow turns solidly positive — the 6-inch cost lead converts to margin and the capex bet de-risks.
- **→ LOW if**: FY27 non-GAAP GM stalls in the low-40s while 6-inch share of InP rises (cost lead fails to reach parametric yield), OR a hyperscaler publicly qualifies a competitor's CPO laser over COHR's, OR FQ1'27 revenue misses the $2.2B low end.
- **→ CLOSE if**: NVIDIA discloses (or guidance implies) a rebalancing of optical volume toward Lumentum post-Greensboro AND COHR datacenter growth decelerates below 20% YoY while FCF stays negative — the #2 loses the anchor without having earned margin independence.

# [97/159] Theses/CRWD - CrowdStrike Holdings.md
**thesis · ticker: CRWD · status: monitoring · conviction: medium · sector: Cybersecurity · date: 2026-04-22 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

A single 40MB kernel-level agent distributes 30 modules across endpoint, identity, browser, AI workloads, and cloud runtime, a substrate PANW's five-pillar federation cannot replicate through acquisition because architectural purity cannot be retrofitted. Consensus treats CRWD as the premium pure-play. The non-consensus view is that three structural moats are mispriced in the current 18x forward revenue multiple: (1) the single-agent architecture is a compounding cost advantage, not a marketing feature: every new module ships on an agent the customer already runs while PANW requires separate install/deployment/console per pillar; (2) the July 2024 outage was the largest switching-cost test in enterprise SaaS history and the moat passed (>95% contract retention across 8.5M crashed systems, RPO +36% YoY), and the market models recovered-risk when the empirical evidence is structural entrenchment proof; (3) Falcon Flex ($1.69B ARR, +120% YoY, 27% of ending ARR) is consumption-based pricing dressed as subscription, structurally eliminating the platformization-tax margin compression PANW is paying for. Layered on top, a 12-24 month agentic-AI lead (Charlotte AI + AIDR shipped; PANW's Precision AI is primarily assistant-mode) creates the "AI-native security" mindshare position the way Snowflake owned "cloud data warehouse." At ~$191B market cap (~18x forward revenue, -4% FY26 GAAP operating margin scaling to 24%+ FY27 guide, 26%→30%+ FCF guide), continued execution is priced at a materially higher bar than at thesis inception; the structural asymmetry is not. Critical risk: any second reliability incident collapses the entrenchment narrative; Wiz structurally caps Falcon Cloud Security upside; Microsoft E3/E5 bundling limits mid-market TAM extension.

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ HIGH if**: CRWD posts 4+ consecutive quarters post-July-2024 with zero material reliability incidents (>1M systems or >$50M damages) AND FY27 ARR growth remains >22% through Q2 FY27 AND Falcon Flex new-logo growth is >25% of total Flex adds (confirms non-pull-forward durability).
- **→ LOW if**: Any new material reliability incident occurs (customer impact >1M systems OR >$50M damages) OR Falcon Flex ARR growth decelerates below 50% YoY for 2 consecutive quarters (pulling-forward thesis confirmed) OR net new ARR decelerates below +10% YoY for 2 consecutive quarters.
- **→ CLOSE if**: Net retention drops below 115% for 2 consecutive quarters (signals platform saturation + competitive displacement) OR any material reliability incident occurs within 12 months of a prior incident (reliability risk becomes structural).

# [100/159] Theses/CSU - Constellation Software.md
**thesis · ticker: CSU · status: draft · conviction: high · sector: Vertical Market Software · date: 2026-05-22 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

The AI-disruption sell-off (peak-to-trough -51%, recovering to -33% by May 2026) re-rated CSU from a 22.5x EV/EBITDA decade-median compounder to ~16x on the thesis that vertical market software (VMS) faces the same generative-AI extinction risk as horizontal SaaS. That framing misreads the moat: 65–75% of CSU's portfolio (Harris, Volaris, Topicus pure-VMS) sits in workflows where switching cost is operational and regulatory, not technical, and where the absolute software bill is too small for AI-native challengers to attract venture capital that would justify the multi-year rebuild required to displace incumbent. The genuine non-consensus angle is that the AI-fear narrative creates the best distressed-VMS acquisition vintage in 15 years exactly when CSU has institutionalized two new capital outlets: PEMS minority stakes (Asseco, Sabre) and accelerating spin-out economics (Topicus +38% YTD, Lumine flat at IPO price but FCF up 2.5x). Mark Leonard's September 2025 health-driven resignation and March 2026 board exit (continuing as PEMS advisor) transferred operational authority to 30-year insider Mark Miller without disrupting the decentralized six-operating-group model. Q1 2026 deployed $1.59B in 135 days, matching full-year 2025, at unchanged IRR hurdles. The risk is that organic growth is genuinely decelerating (maintenance recurring 4% FXN in Q1 2026 vs 6% Q4 2025 vs 5–6% historical average) and NOPAT ROIC has compressed from 49% (2023) to 33.5% (2024), so the model is still working but with less margin of safety than the AI-bear-takes-down-stock-50% reaction implies.

## Conviction Triggers

```
→ HIGH if: (a) Maintenance recurring organic growth re-accelerates to ≥5% FXN in either Q2 or Q3 2026
           (confirming the Q1 2026 deceleration was cyclical not structural) AND
           (b) Full-year 2026 capital deployment exceeds $2.5B at unchanged hurdle rates AND
           (c) At least one additional PEMS deal closes at favorable terms beyond Sabre/Asseco.
           These three conditions collectively confirm the AI-fear narrative is empirically wrong
           AND the capital-deployment + PEMS engine is scaling, justifying multiple recovery to 20x+.

→ LOW if: Maintenance recurring organic growth remains ≤4% FXN through both Q2 and Q3 2026
          (two consecutive prints confirming structural compression) OR
          NOPAT ROIC compresses below 25% in full-year 2026 (vs 33.5% in 2024) OR
          Mark Miller publicly signals lower hurdle rate acceptance on a category of deals
          (the "magnetism effect" begins manifesting).
          Any of these signals the compounder framework is breaking; downside to 12–14x EV/EBITDA
          with degraded FCFA2S growth = -20% to -30% from current.

→ CLOSE if: Maintenance recurring organic growth turns negative for two consecutive quarters
            (confirming AI substitution as a structural pricing-power destroyer) OR
            NOPAT ROIC compresses below 20% with capital deployment dropping below $1.0B/year
            (confirming the model is no longer working at scale) OR
            A material PEMS investment (>$500M) is written down or sold at a loss
            (confirming PEMS as a strategy is broken).
            Any of these confirms the value-trap outcome; reallocate capital.
```

**thesis · ticker: FORM · status: monitoring · conviction: medium · sector: Semiconductor Capital Equipment · date: 2026-04-29 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

FormFactor is the only US-listed pure-play on the HBM4 test-intensity inflection: probe cards are consumables disguised as capex equipment, and the unit-economics shift from HBM3 (12-Hi) to HBM4 (16-Hi) puts FORM in a structural position the sell-side is modeling as cyclical. Probe-card pin count scales superlinearly with stack height because every TSV requires a known-good-die test before stack assembly, and HBM4's wider 2,048-bit interface compounds the increase. The ~226% LTM rally has priced in the first-derivative HBM upgrade narrative; the non-consensus claim is that the second-derivative (test-step density inside each stack, not just stack count) widens FORM's revenue per HBM wafer at a multiple the consensus model treats as flat. Conviction is medium not high because (i) trailing 130x P/E leaves no valuation cushion if Q1 2026 prints in-line rather than blow-out, (ii) Technoprobe is taking ~30% of TSMC 2nm probe-card share at the foundry layer, and (iii) the same AI capex cycle that's the bull case is also the systemic risk if hyperscaler digestion arrives in 2H 2026.

## Conviction Triggers

**→ HIGH if**:
- Q1 2026 print shows HBM segment growth >45% YoY AND HBM4 design-win disclosure includes Samsung + SK Hynix + Micron all qualified, AND
- Technoprobe Q2 2026 disclosure shows memory-segment revenue <10% of total, AND
- FORM announces $200M+ buyback authorization within 90 days

**→ LOW if**:
- Q1 2026 print shows HBM segment growth <25% YoY OR gross margin <40%, OR
- Technoprobe announces HBM advanced-packaging probe-card design win at TSMC CoWoS-L within 12 months, OR
- FORM management acknowledges customer pushback on HBM4 probe-card pricing on any earnings call within 12 months

**→ CLOSE if**:
- Stock price reaches $80+ before HBM4 ramp evidence (overshoot above bull-case fair value), OR
- Hyperscaler aggregate data-center capex guidance cuts >15% YoY for FY27 with <2 quarters of warning, OR
- FORM loses >2 of 3 HBM4 sockets at SK Hynix/Samsung/Micron to Technoprobe or MJC

# [107/159] Theses/GLD - SPDR Gold Shares.md
**thesis · ticker: GLD · status: monitoring · conviction: medium · sector: Precious Metals · date: 2026-04-24 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

> Instrument: SPDR Gold Shares (GLD): $165B AUM, 0.40% expense, HSBC London-vault custody. Used as the investable proxy for a physical-gold thesis. Alternative vehicles (IAU 0.25%, GLDM 0.10%, PHYS closed-end, allocated bullion) discussed in §4.

## Summary

Physical gold is in the early innings of a structural regime change, not a late-cycle commodity rally. Two compounding forces drive it: (1) central banks replacing USD reserves with gold at 750–850 t/yr run-rate (roughly double the pre-2022 average, corrected 2026-07-10 from "10×"; 2025 actual ~863t), and (2) US fiscal trajectory toward $45T debt forcing fiscal dominance that caps real yields below structural inflation. [Corrected 2026-07-10: the original third force ("Basel III Tier 1 HQLA recognition effective July 2025") was struck as factually void; gold has never held HQLA status under Basel liquidity rules (LBMA published an explicit correction of this meme). See Insight #4.] The current $4,400–4,800 range is a 20% cyclical retrace from the $5,595 January 2026 ATH, driven by Warsh-era Fed hawkishness and Iran-induced dollar strength, the same setup as the 1974 mid-cycle retrace that preceded gold's 1975–1980 165% run. GLD at $435.26 offers asymmetric exposure with JPM, BofA, UBS, and Deutsche targets clustered at $5,400–6,300 (15–35% upside) and credible bull-case path to $7,000+ if Fed pivots and BRICS settlement architecture scales.

## Conviction Triggers

**→ HIGH if:**
- Fed cuts rates at May or June 2026 FOMC AND core services CPI prints below 3.0% annualized (confirms fiscal-dominance pivot path)
- OR: BRICS settlement unit extends to 3+ actively-settling members with $10B+ monthly trade volume by YE 2026
- OR: PBOC publicly revises official reserves upward by >500t (signals willingness to acknowledge accumulation, typically precedes acceleration)

**→ LOW if:**
- Brent falls below $70 AND DXY sustained >108 for 60+ days AND 10Y real yield breaches 2.5% (triple-barrel macro headwind)
- OR: Two consecutive monthly PBOC reserve prints show zero or negative gold additions (17-month buying streak breaks)
- OR: Gold ETF outflows exceed $25B in a single month globally (capitulation signal)

**→ CLOSE if:**
- Gold falls below $3,500 on sustained dollar strength AND credible US fiscal consolidation (e.g., bipartisan deficit reduction plan passes Congress)
- OR: Fed hikes to >5.25% and core services CPI holds below 2.5% for 3 consecutive months (Volcker-analog credibility restoration invalidates debasement thesis)
- OR: BRICS settlement unit pilot collapses publicly AND PBOC announces a pause in gold accumulation

EBITDA (44% margin), $133M FCF. At ~$17.34 (~$3.1B mcap, ~15x FY26E EV/EBITDA, ~6x forward sales) it screens cheap for 20%+ growth. The non-consensus point is that the durable-monopoly framing is probably wrong in the direction that matters, not the short-squeeze the WSB crowd is pitching: individual switching costs are near-zero, management is strip-mining the network via "enshittification" (ad flood + paywalls) to drive price-led ARPPU, a now-Match-funded Sniffies is taking share, and the paying-user metric the entire multiple rests on is the subject of an alleged undisclosed SEC probe (NINGI Research, Sept 2025, into APU double-counting). The $18 Zage/Lu take-private that once floored the stock was withdrawn March 2026. This is a convex, downside-bounded trade on a genuinely profitable asset with an unverified core metric, sized as optionality, not a compounder. Conviction low until the APU/SEC question resolves and paying-user growth proves volume-led rather than price-led.

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ HIGH if**: Q2/Q3 2026 prints show APU growth is volume-led (unit growth ≥ ARPPU growth) AND EDGE early-cohort data shows ≥0.5% MAU attach at ≥$100/mo ARPU AND no adverse SEC/APU disclosure through FY26 audit — i.e., the metric is clean and the monetization runway is genuine.
- **→ LOW if**: APU growth decelerates below 10% YoY OR a second consecutive quarter of MAU/download decline while Sniffies/Scruff accelerate (multi-homing tipping liquidity) OR any confirmed SEC inquiry into APU is disclosed.
- **→ CLOSE if**: an SEC action or restatement alleges material APU misstatement (metric integrity broken) OR MAU declines year-over-year (the liquidity monopoly is actually cracking, network effect running in reverse).

# [110/159] Theses/HOOD - Robinhood Markets.md
**thesis · ticker: HOOD · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: Retail Brokerage & Fintech Platforms · date: 2026-08-04 · metrics as of: 2026-08-04**

## Summary

Robinhood at ~$92 (~$82.5B market cap, ~40x trailing P/E, ~15x EV/FY26E revenue) is priced by the market as a high-beta retail-trading proxy: a bet on volatile options/crypto/event-contract volumes that mean-revert with risk appetite, as the −51% round-trip from $147 to $71.83 in Feb–Apr 2026 demonstrated. The non-consensus thesis is that underneath the cyclical transaction line (59% of Q2 revenue), a compounding platform-asset annuity is forming (net deposits +27% TTM to $75.7B, total platform assets $369B (+32% YoY), ARPU $187 (+24%), Gold subscribers 4.8M (+39%)) that is quietly converting trading customers into net-interest, subscription, and advisory payers whose revenue is far less cyclical. Robinhood is simultaneously the highest-operating-leverage brokerage ever built (57% adjusted-EBITDA margin, near-fully-automated stack running $369B and 28.4M customers on a fraction of incumbent headcount) and is porting that automation into 13 business lines now exceeding $100M annualized (up from one in 2019): banking, advisory (Strategies/Cortex), the Gold/Platinum cards, prediction markets, tokenization, and federally-seeded Trump Accounts. Conviction is **medium**, not high: the ~40x multiple prices in continued flawless execution, and the genuine risk is that the "diversified" transaction mix is really one correlated retail-risk-appetite factor that collapses together in a risk-off while Fed cuts simultaneously compress net interest income: a live 40%+ drawdown scenario the early-2026 tape already rehearsed.

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ HIGH if:** across a quarter where transaction revenue falls ≥15% QoQ, total revenue still grows YoY because NII + subscription + advisory + banking more than offset (i.e., the platform annuity demonstrably de-cyclicalizes the model) **and** net deposits sustain ≥25% growth.
- **→ LOW if:** a broad retail risk-off shows options + event contracts + equities + crypto falling together >30% in a single quarter (proving one correlated factor), **or** net-deposit growth decelerates below ~15% while adj-EBITDA margin compresses below ~45%.
- **→ CLOSE if:** an adverse regulatory outcome structurally impairs the model: a US PFOF ban with no viable replacement, **or** a CFTC/federal ruling forcing event-contract withdrawal in major states, **and** the multiple has not yet de-rated to reflect it (the risk is un-priced).

# [111/159] Theses/INTC - Intel.md
**thesis · ticker: INTC · status: monitoring · conviction: low · sector: Compute & AI Compute Accelerators · date: 2026-04-27 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

Intel under Lip-Bu Tan (CEO since March 2025) is the most consequential turnaround story in semiconductors, and the market has rerated another 30–60% on top of the earlier rally. The stock closed ~$109 May 9 inside a 52-week range of $18.97–$133.48 against a $585–628B market cap (data-provider variance post Nvidia/SoftBank/USG issuance), trading at ~117x forward P/E vs TSMC 22x and AMD 45x. Q1 2026 printed $13.58B revenue (sixth consecutive beat, +7% YoY) and $0.29 non-GAAP EPS (+123% YoY) on 41.0% non-GAAP gross margin (+180bps) and 12.3% non-GAAP operating margin (+690bps); GAAP EPS was $(0.73) after a $4.07B Mobileye goodwill impairment. Q2 2026 guidance bracketed $13.8–14.8B revenue (consensus $13.06B) with $0.20 EPS vs consensus $0.06: magnitude of the beats is the principal sentiment-positive datapoint. CFO Zinsner disclosed at Q1 that 18A yields are tracking to hit the year-end-2026 cost target by mid-2026 (two quarters ahead of plan, ~7%/month gain per Pitzer RBC TMT Nov 2025); revised EMIB external packaging revenue guidance from "a few hundred million" to >$1B annually per customer ("close to closing some deals that are in the billions", Morgan Stanley TMT, March 2026). Apple preliminary multi-year 14A deal reported by WSJ May 8 catalyzed a 14% single-day rally. The bull narrative is now comprehensive: 18A entered HVM Oct 2025 with Microsoft Maia 2/3 ("Griffin") on 18A/18A-P under a $15B contract + AWS AI Fabric; Tesla committed to 14A in April 2026 (Terafab, 2029 first volume); Nvidia took a $5B equity stake (4.4%) in December 2025 for Xeon-6-as-Vera-Rubin-NVL72-host-CPU; SoftBank took $2B at $23 (Aug 2025); the US government holds 10% (433.3M shares at $20.47, $8.9B basis) plus a 5-year warrant for 5% additional shares at $20; agentic-AI CPU TAM expansion + ASIC revenue >$1B run-rate (doubled YoY Q1 2026) reframe DCAI as AI infrastructure; Intel closed the $14.2B buyback of Apollo's 49% Fab 34 (Ireland) minority on April 1, 2026 funded $7.7B cash + $6.5B new debt. The non-consensus tension intensifies as the rally extends. The Jaykihn leak (April 2026) shows Diamond Rapids slipped from 2026 to mid-2027, ceding another full P-core server cycle to AMD Venice (Zen 6, TSMC N2, on track 2026) while Intel's first competitive SMT-restored P-core Xeon (Coral Rapids) does not arrive until mid-2028; AMD took record Q4 2025 server share at 28.8% units / 41.3% revenue (Mercury), first quarter ever above 40% with a 12.5-point unit-vs-revenue gap signalling AMD wins the highest-margin SKUs. >90% of Intel's own Nova Lake (2027 client) is on TSMC N2 per SemiWiki/Mark Webb, confirming Zinsner's prior Citi admission that Intel "will be putting products on TSMC, you know, forever, really"; External Foundry Q1 2026 was $174M (up from $31M Q1 2025) vs internal $5.2B, so external is still 3.2% of segment revenue. A new shareholder lawsuit (Paisner, filed March 2025, Bloomberg Law coverage, names Lutnick + Yeary) seeks to void the USG 10% stake on fiduciary-breach grounds tied to Trump's August 7, 2025 demand for Tan's resignation: remote but discrete legal overhang. Sell-side consensus target sits at ~$66 (range $25–$118); HSBC's $95 (the most bullish target) is ~15% below the May 9 close; SOTP arithmetic yields $43–73/share, lower-bound 60%+ below current. The 117x forward multiple at ~$110–130 implies $1.20–1.50 2028 EPS, requiring DCAI 15–20% sustained growth + foundry op-margin breakeven + 35%+ gross margin: a near-perfect outcome path. Conviction medium with bearish lean strengthening: government backstop still bounds downside but the additional 30–60% rally has pushed the valuation gap to its widest of the cycle; the first execution stumble (18A yield disappointment, an IFS customer slip, Diamond Rapids further delay) now compresses the multiple from a 117x base, with the AMD 50% server-share trigger likely arriving sooner given the 41.3% Q4 2025 print.

---

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ HIGH if**: **Mid-2026 (Q2 earnings) discloses 18A yield cost-target hit per CFO Zinsner guide** (yield ≥75% confirmed) AND Microsoft Maia 2 commercial launch on 18A confirmed AND Q1 2027 14A external customer roster expands beyond Tesla (any of Apple Pro/Max/Ultra, Nvidia primary GPU dies, or Google formally committing) AND external Foundry quarterly revenue exceeds $500M wafer + $500M packaging: all four required, not any. The Apple preliminary multi-year deal (WSJ May 8, 2026) is preliminary; HIGH conviction requires firm volume + node confirmation, not the announcement itself.

- **→ LOW if**: Q4 2026 earnings disclose 18A yield <70% by end-2026 OR Microsoft / AWS reduces 18A wafer commitments OR **AMD EPYC server share crosses 50% in any quarter through 2026 (now a 2026 base case given 41.3% Q4 2025 print, not a tail outcome)** OR external Foundry revenue stalls below $200M/qtr (vs $174M Q1 2026 trajectory).

- **→ CLOSE if**: 14A development is publicly slowed or cancelled by H1 2027 (per Lip-Bu Tan's own conditional framing) OR Coral Rapids architectural disclosure slips beyond 2028 OR IFS Q4 2026 quarterly loss exceeds $3B (margin deterioration despite revenue scaling) OR Paisner shareholder lawsuit succeeds in voiding the USG 10% stake (removes structural floor).

# [115/159] Theses/IREN - IREN Limited.md
**thesis · ticker: IREN · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: Neoclouds & GPU-as-a-Service · date: 2026-08-04**

## Summary
The market prices IREN in one of two wrong buckets: a levered NVIDIA-derivative GPU-renter (like [[Theses/CRWV - CoreWeave]]), or a Bitcoin miner about to revert to commodity power-arbitrage multiples; both pin the durable asset in the wrong place. IREN's mispriced layer is the **5.8 GW of self-developed, self-owned, grid-interconnected power + land + energized liquid-cooled shell** it built cheaply during the Bitcoin era, now the exact toll layer the AI build is bottlenecked on. Vertical ownership of that layer produces ~90% AI-cloud gross margins and an estimated ~85% project EBITDA on the Microsoft contract, structurally above the lease-and-DDTL neocloud model, and, as the vault's own hedge note concedes, makes IREN a "poor short" and an anti-hedge that can re-rate up in a GPU-supply shock. That asymmetry (revert-to-power-arbitrage floor beneath, power-scarcity convexity above) is the whole thesis. The catch is the path: a ~$21 B estimated funding gap to build the pipeline out, inside the AI-capex "fragile tranche," against a probable 2028–29 digestion. This is a downside-bounded, dilution-exposed convex bet ([[Generalist - Overview]] [G-9] barbell tail), not a core compounder, hence **medium conviction, draft**.

## Conviction Triggers
- **→ HIGH if**: IREN energizes >1 GW of contracted AI-cloud capacity at sustained >80% project EBITDA **and** bridges ≥12 months of the build with <15% cumulative share-count dilution, proving the power floor funds the ramp rather than diluting through it.
- **→ LOW if**: any single 6-month window shows >20% share-count dilution, **or** a GPU re-rent/utilization disclosure shows >30% rate compression, **or** Microsoft/NVIDIA reduces committed capacity.
- **→ CLOSE if**: a contracted hyperscaler counterparty cancels or defers a major tranche **and** IREN breaches liquidity to fund committed GPU purchases (forcing a distressed sale of power assets, the event that proves the floor was a narrative).

**thesis · ticker: KLA · status: active · conviction: high · sector: Semiconductor Capital Equipment · date: 2026-04-29 · metrics as of: 2026-08-15**

## Summary

KLA is the only WFE business with two compounding growth vectors instead of one: wafer-fab-equipment dollars and process-control intensity (PC% of WFE rising 100-200bp every node from 12% at 16/14nm to projected 16-17% at 2nm and 17-19% at 1.4nm), sitting on top of a 56-63% process-control share that has risen through every architectural transition in the past 15 years. FY26 printed $13.58B (+11.7%), $1.3B below the $14.9B / +16-18% the April thesis carried, so the ~14-16% CAGR floor through 2030 is now a FY27–30 catch-up claim against a ~9-11% WFE consensus, not a trailing fact. The stock is $204 / $266B after the Jun 12 10-for-1: 55x LTM / 55x NTM, 37x FY27E $5.48, and the 30-36x "quality-cheap vs Visa/Moody's" inversion does not hold on delivered earnings; residual cheapness is only the street's +49% FY27 EPS step-up. Layered on top: a ~$3.1B (23% mix) service annuity guided to ~$6B by 2030 (13-15% CAGR, capex-cycle-insensitive; ~$47-56B today at 25-30x service EBITDA, ~18-21% of cap, not the $80-100B / 35-40% the April mix implied) and an advanced-packaging franchise that grew 70% YoY to $950M in FY2025 with bond-line inspection categories that did not exist three years ago. 73% ROIC, 61% gross margin, 42% operating margin, 17 consecutive years of dividend increases, $7B buyback authorization (~2.6% of cap): operationally the highest-quality compounder in the equipment universe, structurally a hidden subscription business in a cyclical wrapper, now priced as one.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if** any 3 of 4: 2nm GAA HVM tool-of-record selections at TSMC confirm KLA share gain ≥150bp at process control + Q4 FY26 print delivers AP segment ≥$1.4B run-rate (+47% YoY from $950M FY25) + service revenue Q4 FY26 ≥$1.05B (+15%+ YoY) + buyback execution Q4 FY26 ≥$2B in single quarter signaling front-loaded deployment.

→ **LOW if** any one: TSMC discloses ML-based virtual metrology displacing ≥10% of KLA inspection passes at N3/N2 in any earnings call OR China revenue exits FY26 below 18% of mix (vs 22% FY25) with explicit Skyverse/Camtek displacement disclosure at SMIC OR AP segment Q2 FY26 reports flat sequential growth (broken acceleration trajectory) OR Q4 FY26 FY27 guide implies revenue growth ≤8% (vs implicit 15%+ to hit $26B 2030 target).

→ **CLOSE if** simultaneous: process-control share drops below 50% in any KLAC disclosure (structural breach of monopoly assumption) + service revenue CAGR slips below 8% on rolling 4-quarter basis + Investor Day target formally reset downward to $20-22B 2030 + multiple compression to 22-25x sustained for ≥6 months. Equivalent: thesis decomposition across all 5 non-consensus pillars.

# [119/159] Theses/LITE - Lumentum.md
**thesis · ticker: LITE · status: active · conviction: high · sector: Optical Networking & Photonics · date: 2026-04-03 · metrics as of: 2026-08-16**

## Summary

Lumentum occupies arguably the single most strategically important chokepoint in the AI infrastructure buildout: a near-monopoly in 200G-per-lane electro-absorption modulated lasers (EMLs) required for every 1.6T transceiver and co-packaged optics module deployed in hyperscale data centers. With an estimated 50-60% share of high-speed EMLs and sole volume production at 200G per lane, Lumentum is sold out through the end of 2027, with demand exceeding supply by 25-30% even as the company executes an 8x capacity expansion from FY2023 levels. The revenue trajectory, $1.65B (FY2025) to $3.01B (FY2026, +83% vs the prior $2.94B guide), with FQ1'27 at $1.225–1.275B / 39.5–40.5% non-GAAP OM (the old late-2027 40% OM target is now the next-quarter guide) and a $2B quarterly run-rate still targeted by late 2027, reflects the extreme operating leverage inherent in a physics-gated monopoly supplier. NVIDIA's $2B strategic investment in March 2026, S&P 500 inclusion, and a $400M+ optical circuit switch backlog validate the structural demand thesis. The core non-consensus insight is that Lumentum functions as an "arms dealer" in the photonics supercycle: even when it loses a module contract, the winner often sources EML chips from Lumentum, creating component-level pricing power that persists through every architectural transition (pluggable, linear drive, and co-packaged optics). At ~$926 (~$72B, ~50x forward P/E, PEG ~0.40 on Street FY27 EPS $18.58), PEG still says growth is not fully in the multiple, but ~50x already prices OFC-model delivery after the first $1B quarter, ~15% below the $1,086 ATH, execution risk at this scaling velocity still material.

**thesis · ticker: LPKF · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: ABF Substrates & Advanced Packaging Supply Chain · date: 2026-06-07 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

LPKF is the dominant supplier of the laser process, Laser-Induced Deep Etching (LIDE), that the entire next-generation advanced-packaging glass-substrate stack depends on for through-glass-vias (TGVs), and the consensus model under-counts the per-line content by ~3× because the Street still treats LPKF as a single-tool TGV drilling vendor when management has actually disclosed a four-step laser process line (drilling + multilayer bonding + singulation + CPO waveguide structuring) priced at ~€13M up-front plus €1.5–2M annual recurring per line. The market is pricing a binary near-term execution risk (€5.8M cash, CEO-promised "production orders by end Q2 2026" at June-4 AGM, activist shareholders demanding capital raise CEO refuses) into a stock that, if 2027 LIDE production ramp materialises on schedule, sits in the only proven LIDE-at-scale duopoly globally (LPKF + Plan Optik) with patent defence affirmed in EU (Apr-2025) + Korea (Sep-2025) for a glass-substrate end-market that Intel + Samsung + TSMC + DNP + Rapidus are simultaneously committing 5–10 year mass-production roadmaps to. Conviction is medium not high because the balance-sheet runway is thin (3–4 quarters at current burn absent a financing event), the timing distance between the equipment-vendor S-curve (LPKF needs tool orders 2026–2027) and the substrate-customer S-curve (TSMC 2028–29, Samsung 2027, Intel 2030) is wider than management's reassurance suggests, and the Solar segment cyclical collapse compounds the operating loss while the CEO is simultaneously refusing the dilution that would buy execution-time optionality.

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ HIGH if**: By end Q3 2026, LPKF discloses 2+ LIDE production orders at Tier-1 named customers (any combination of Samsung Electro-Mechanics, Absolics, Intel, DNP, TSMC, Rapidus, JNTC) at unit counts totalling 6+ tools and ASPs consistent with disclosed €3.5–5M LIDE-A range, AND cash position holds above €4M without bridge financing required. Validates equipment-S-curve timing model and removes near-term distress risk.

- **→ LOW if**: Q2 2026 LIDE production orders slip past 30-Sep-2026 OR cash falls below €3M without committed bridge financing facility OR Plan Optik (or any other LIDE-equivalent competitor) wins a publicly-disclosed Tier-1 customer evaluation that LPKF was competing for.

- **→ CLOSE if**: LIDE core patent successfully invalidated in any major jurisdiction (EU, Korea, US, Japan) OR a major substrate customer (Intel, TSMC, Samsung) publicly de-prioritises glass-core substrate investment in favour of organic ABF extension OR LPKF management announces a dilutive capital raise at sub-€15 strike (signals distressed financing rather than growth investment).

# [122/159] Theses/LRCX - Lam Research.md
**thesis · ticker: LRCX · status: active · conviction: high · sector: Semiconductor Capital Equipment · date: 2026-04-29 · metrics as of: 2026-08-15**

## Summary

Lam is being priced as a memory-cycle WFE vendor levered to NAND/DRAM capex restart, but its Q3 FY26 print revealed a 59% Foundry/Logic revenue mix, a structural shift from "memory cyclical" to "logic+memory diversified" that the market has repriced through the multiple, not the beta: NTM P/E compressed 43x → ~35x (FY27 $9.38) after FY26 printed $5.79 / $23.2B (filed Aug 7) even as the stock held ~$332; LTM is 57x; beta still 1.78. Beneath that mix shift, Lam holds four independent content-per-wafer expansion vectors that compound rather than substitute: ~80% etch share at sub-3nm GAA via the Akara platform, dry resist Aether as production tool of record at SK Hynix (first commercial productization of a 5+yr R&D bet), Cryo 3.0 + ALTUS Halo Mo ALD for 400-1000 layer NAND, and a +40% advanced packaging guide for CY26 making Lam a full-stack AP vendor (TSV etch via Vantex, copper plating via SABRE 3D, hybrid bonding via CBA NAND). Management's 2028 IDay target of $25-27B revenue / 50% GM / 34-35% OM / $6-7 EPS implies structural margin expansion, not cyclical mean reversion. Lam is becoming the etch+deposition counterparty to ASML's lithography monopoly, but the market is still grading it on memory beta.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if**: Aether wins second tool-of-record customer (TSMC, Micron, or Samsung) by Q1 CY27 AND NAND capex restart confirmed at 400-layer scale by Q3 CY26 AND Q3 FY26 mix shift (59% Foundry/Logic) holds through Q1 FY27 print. Three legs of the structural thesis confirm in sequence.

→ **HIGH if**: Q4 FY26 print (Aug 2026) shows AP revenue >$1.4B AND CY26 raised WFE forecast holds at ≥$140B AND CSBG holds within 5% of Q3 FY26 trend. Validates AP full-stack thesis + IDay glide tracking + service durability simultaneously.

→ **LOW if**: Aether shows zero new customer disclosure for 18+ months past Q3 FY26 print (i.e., no new TOR by Oct CY27) OR Q4 FY26 China revenue >32% (failure of <30% guide indicating slower geopolitical glide).

→ **LOW if**: TSMC or Samsung publicly switches to AMAT/TEL selective etch tool for any sub-3nm GAA step, breaking the ~80% Akara share narrative.

→ **CLOSE if**: Multiple-compression on NAND restart slip + China cliff combination produces 2 consecutive quarters of revenue decline AND IDay 2028 targets formally lowered by management at any IR event. Thesis falsified on structural-cycle and category-share dimensions simultaneously.

→ **CLOSE if**: Forward P/E drops to <22x on a sector-wide derate, signaling that the structural thesis premium has been priced out — re-evaluate at lower multiple as a different (value cyclical) thesis rather than holding through the regime shift.
