# [123/159] Theses/LYV - Live Nation Entertainment.md
**thesis · ticker: LYV · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: Live Entertainment & Ticketing · date: 2026-07-14**

## Summary

The moat and the legal liability are now the same asset, and the market stopped pricing the liability at the exact moment a jury confirmed it. LYV traded ~$70–90 in 2024 on breakup fear; at ~$183 (July 2026, an all-time high, ~$42.6B market cap, ~18x EV/AOI) it prices the March 2026 Trump-DOJ settlement (15% fee cap, 13 amphitheater divestitures, no breakup) as the operative outcome and declares antitrust risk "fading." That narrative ignores that on April 15, 2026 a federal jury (33 states + DC, who rejected the settlement) found Live Nation an illegal monopoly on **all** counts, and the remedy phase before Judge Subramanian has structural separation of Ticketmaster explicitly on the table. The market has collapsed a genuinely bimodal outcome to a point estimate. Underneath the legal noise sits a compounding, AI-agnostic experiential-demand machine: FY2025 revenue $25.2B (+9%), AOI $2.4B (+10%), record Q1-2026 deferred revenue $6.6B (+22%), international fan count exceeding US for the first time, with ~90% of profit in the 37%-margin Ticketing toll and 64%-margin Sponsorship layers, not the 3%-margin Concerts volume the market anchors on. This is an event-driven special situation, not a clean compounder mispricing: the layer analysis is worthless without the remedy-phase handicap attached.

## Conviction Triggers

```
→ HIGH if: Judge Subramanian approves the DOJ settlement (Tunney Act) AND declines
   structural separation in the state remedy phase (behavioral-only remedy),
   removing the breakup tail — WITH Ticketing AOI margin holding >33% through 2026
   (fee cap non-binding). Overhang gone + flywheel intact.

→ HIGH if: a structural-separation order arrives AND a credible SOTP (standalone
   Ticketmaster >15x EV/AOI + standalone Concerts/Venue Nation) values the parts
   >20% above the current bundled EV — the feared event becomes the catalyst.

→ LOW if: Ticketing AOI margin compresses >500bps across 2026–2027 on the fee cap +
   open-ticketing share loss with no international/sponsorship offset, OR full-year
   AOI growth falls below high-single-digits (flywheel stalling).

→ CLOSE if: a structural-separation remedy is ordered AND upheld on terms that impair
   both halves (divestiture below SOTP value + multi-year operational disruption),
   OR experiential demand rolls over (fan attendance declines YoY), confirming
   post-boom mean-reversion.
```

# [125/159] Theses/MRVL - Marvell Technology.md
**thesis · ticker: MRVL · status: active · conviction: medium · sector: Custom Silicon & Networking Semiconductors · date: 2026-04-23 · metrics as of: 2026-08-15**

## Summary

At $222.02 (Aug 14 close, ~$194B cap) MRVL is -33% off its June 22 peak of $329.88 after a complete derate round-trip: the July capex-reset selloff (ASML's warning, then TSMC pairing a raised ~40% revenue-growth outlook with a capex reset to $60–64B that compresses hyperscaler-FCF math, plus an Erste Buy→Hold on valuation and customer concentration) ran from $188.30 (Jul 16) to $163.40 (Jul 29, -50% off peak), then the FMS AI-memory portfolio launch (Aug 4, +13% single day) and hyperscaler 2026 capex raises (Alphabet $205B, Amazon ~$220B) retraced +36% in two weeks; no thesis pillar moved in either leg. The business itself ran ahead of the April thesis: record Q1 FY27 $2.418B (+28%, DC $1.83B / 76% of revenue), Q2 guide $2.7B (+35%), FY27 target $11.5B, FY28 $16.5B, custom silicon >$10B by FY29 from ~$1.5B FY26. The structure of the case is a three-engine story: a **rented** custom-ASIC second seat (procurement slot, ~60% GM ceiling, seats churn: Trainium 3 went to Alchip, Microsoft is talking to Broadcom on Maia, Google runs Marvell fourth behind Broadcom/MediaTek), an **owned** electro-optics layer (80%+ 800G DSP, the one durable franchise), and a **bought** memory-fabric option (Celestial + XConn + Polariton, three fabric/photonics bolt-ons in five months) that is the single largest binary in the thesis. The August rally has un-priced the July derate: 55x FY27E / 36x FY28E on consensus EPS of $4.05/$6.24, back at the Jul-9 euphoria forward multiple (54x), with consensus FY28 revenue ($16.7B) now ~1% above management's $16.5B target. Medium conviction: the froth leg the derate had cleared is partially rebuilt, and the fundamental variance (custom-seat economics, Celestial validation by 2027–2028, digestion vs regime on capex) lands unresolved into the Aug 27 Q2 print.

## Conviction Triggers

**→ HIGH if** all three of these materialise within FY27:
1. Marvell Q2 or Q3 FY27 earnings confirms signed Google custom silicon contract (not talks, not MOU — a disclosed commercial agreement) for the MPU or inference TPU; AND
2. AWS publicly or via supplier filings confirms Marvell-packaged Trainium 3 allocation of ≥400K units in production; AND
3. Celestial Photonic Fabric confirms the tier-1 Gen-1 selection with a disclosed PO or on-schedule end-2026 tape-out milestone (plus any second named customer = upgrade accelerant).

**→ LOW if** any two of these materialise within FY27:
1. Q2 or Q3 FY27 custom silicon revenue misses guidance by >10%; OR
2. Google signs BRCM-exclusive or BRCM+MediaTek-only extension, formally closing Marvell out of the inference-silicon opportunity; OR
3. OFC 2027 LPO deployment evidence shows >35% 1.6T short-reach share loss from DSP to LPO (confirming rapid ASP compression); OR
4. Celestial integration delays publicly disclosed (Q2 or Q3 FY27 earnings commentary slipping tape-out beyond end-2026).

**→ CLOSE if** any of these materialise:
1. Forward guidance cuts the FY28 custom silicon path below a $3.6B implied run-rate (recalibrated 2026-07-17 from $3B after the May guide raise to >2x YoY off ~$1.8B FY27); OR
2. Celestial integration slips to FY30, triggering goodwill impairment disclosure; OR
3. Trainium 4 socket goes exclusively to Alchip (not Marvell), confirming structural design-partner credibility loss; OR
4. UALink 1.0 wins ≥50% of announced hyperscaler scale-up rack standard commitments for CY28 deployment AND Marvell's XConn/ESUN positions fail to convert (fabric revenue still NVLink-concentrated).

# [128/159] Theses/NBIS - Nebius Group.md
**thesis · ticker: NBIS · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: Neoclouds & GPU-as-a-Service · date: 2026-06-02 · metrics as of: 2026-08-15**

## Summary

Nebius is the same neocloud trade as CoreWeave with the risk vector inverted: CRWV is a credit bet on a fully-built base, NBIS is an execution bet on a base that barely exists yet. The market prices the two as correlated twins (NBIS at ~2.8x CRWV's FY26 EV/revenue, ~20x vs ~7x, on near-parity market caps of ~$67B vs ~$57B), but the binary that decides each is orthogonal: CRWV's thesis turns on counterparty credit and DDTL re-rent economics; NBIS's turns on whether it can physically energize 800MW–1GW of data-center power by YE2026 off a ~170MW YE2025 base, a >4x build in twelve months, to convert $40B of disclosed customer commitments (Microsoft + Meta anchored; ~$50B incl. Meta's flexible tranche) into revenue. Demand is de-risked (capacity sold out, contracted through 2031); supply is not. The non-consensus case is that NBIS is the higher-quality expression of a sector the vault rates bearishly ([[Sectors/Neoclouds & GPU-as-a-Service]]: "neoclouds are levered NVIDIA derivatives, not independent businesses"): cleaner balance sheet (~$8B cash, ~$7–8B of non-core stakes, prepayment-led funding, ~70% of Q2 deals prepaid, plus a first $775M contract-secured facility at SOFR+2.50% rather than GPU-resale DDTL), genuine full-stack software differentiation inherited from Yandex, and embedded venture optionality (ClickHouse ~$4.2B, Avride ~$2.2B) that can self-fund the buildout without the leverage that makes CRWV a credit-risk position. Conviction is medium, not high, because the stock is priced for a flawless 4x build (~120x trailing-FY25 sales; ~$278 spot sits ~23% above the ~$226 consensus PT, below only the freshest post-Q2 raises) and the 2026 funding gap is being closed with a mix that now includes ATM equity ($2.8B issued) and secured debt; medium, not low, because prepayment-led funding and optionality genuinely de-risk it relative to CRWV. Status: draft (sized as a quality-and-optionality position with a hard execution catalyst path, distinct from CRWV's credit framing).

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ HIGH if**: Q3 2026 (Nov) discloses active power ≥600MW on the guided trajectory AND 2026 exit ARR is tracking ≥$8B AND a ClickHouse or Avride monetization event (IPO filing, secondary, or strategic sale) materially de-risks the 2026–2027 funding gap. (All three = the execution-and-funding binary resolves favourably; NBIS becomes the highest-conviction way to own the sector.)
- **→ LOW if**: Q3 2026 active-power build misses guidance by >20%, OR NBIS funds 2026 capex with a >15%-dilutive equity/convert raise in any 6-month window, OR AI-cloud adj EBITDA margin compresses below 35% on a quarterly basis.
- **→ CLOSE if**: YE2026 active-power ramp misses by >30% (backlog conversion mechanically breaks), OR the Meta $15B flexible tranche is amended down / not drawn as MTIA ramps, OR NBIS funds the capex gap primarily via GPU-collateralized debt (the structural differentiation vs [[Theses/CRWV - CoreWeave]] collapses and it should be re-underwritten as a credit-risk position).

# [129/159] Theses/NET - Cloudflare.md
**thesis · ticker: NET · status: active · conviction: medium · sector: Cybersecurity · date: 2026-04-15 · metrics as of: 2026-08-15**

## Summary

330+ city global network with full-stack compute at every PoP creates a "physics moat" (sub-50ms to 95% of the internet-connected population) that widens as AI inference, real-time agents, and voice synthesis tighten latency requirements. Five acquisitions in twelve months (Outerbase, Arroyo, Human Native, Astro, Replicate) assembled the only platform spanning CDN to AI inference to agent infrastructure to content monetization. FY2025: $2.17B revenue (+30%); Q2 2026 (reported Aug 6): $696.1M (+36%, a second straight acceleration), RPO $2.73B (+38%), FY2026 guide raised to $2.864-2.870B (+32%), record 80,000+ paying-customer net adds in the quarter (+74% YoY paying-customer growth). At ~$316 (~$112B market cap, ~45x trailing sales), a reverse DCF requires 25-30% revenue CAGR for 5-7 years while roughly doubling FCF margins from ~15% (TTM) toward the 30%+ management now targets, and the +73% re-rating from the ~$183/$65B level the thesis was written at has widened that already-demanding gap. The June 9, 2026 Investor Day reframed the company around "Act IV", monetizing agentic traffic at the network layer (pay-per-crawl, content marketplace, x402), and **raised the long-term operating model**: operating margin 20%→30%+, FCF margin ~25%→30-35%+, GAAP profitability committed by 2028, and ~$5B annualized revenue reaffirmed before YE2028 (~28% CAGR from $2.4B). Management lowered the gross-margin floor to 70% (from 75%), pivoting investors to a 44% "unit economic margin." The thesis hinges on whether the agentic internet materializes at scale and whether Cloudflare's traffic interception position converts to durable economic capture. The platform moat is genuine and deepening; the question is whether the price already reflects it.

The market got there first: ONTO is +174% over twelve months to ~$336 (~$16.6B cap), re-rated from the ~24x "value or value-trap" laggard of the metrology trio (per the vault's own late-May sector note) to ~46x forward / ~65x trailing, with the sell-side at "Strong Buy" but an average target only ~5% above spot. The investable question is therefore no longer "is ONTO cheap" (that entry is gone) but "does a genuine CoPoS call-option plus 50%+ advanced-packaging growth justify a compounder multiple on the sub-scale, most-contestable member of a field that KLA is actively invading?" Medium conviction: own the structural exposure the vault wants, but at 46x the margin of safety is in the optionality, not the price.

## Summary

Oracle is two businesses the market keeps pricing as one, and it owns the wrong one for the story it is being sold on. The consensus trades Oracle as "the 4th hyperscaler": a single AI-infrastructure growth engine re-rated to a record ~$345 in late 2025 on a $638B RPO, then cut in half (to ~$120, −50%) through 2026 as OpenAI concentration and a −$24B free-cash-flow hole turned euphoria to panic. The risk everyone is trading, the OCI GPU-rental business, is a ~16%-gross-margin, negative-FCF, single-counterparty (OpenAI ≈ half of RPO) layer-renter with neocloud economics. Conviction is medium, not high, because the honest disconfirming case is strong: Oracle's interest/revenue is ~7%, nowhere near CoreWeave's 25.8% break-line, so a 70%-margin database annuity funds the build in a way no neocloud can, and the database layer-monopoly is a genuine long hiding inside the AI-capex fear.

           is largely wrong and Oracle is a genuine hyperscaler. [Insight 1 + 4 falsifiers fire]

FCF $1.220B (63% margin); U.S. commercial RDV $6.238B (+124% YoY); U.S. commercial TCV record $2.132B (+153% YoY). FY2026 guide raised to $8.150–$8.158B (+82%) and U.S. commercial to >$3.424B (≥134%). NDR was disclosed on the call at 157% (+700bps QoQ from Q1's 150%); the hard NRR falsifier now sits 37pp above the ~120% break level; customer count 1,049 (+24% YoY). See [[Research/2026-08-12 - PLTR - Q2 2026 Earnings IR Verification]].

Q4 2025: $1.41B revenue (+70% YoY), U.S. commercial +137%, Rule of 40 at 127%. Q1 2026 extended the beat-and-raise: $1.63B revenue (+85% YoY), U.S. revenue +104%, EPS $0.33, with FY2026 guidance raised to $7.65-7.66B (+71%) and U.S. commercial to +120% (~$4.0B adjusted FCF). Palantir's Ontology architecture, a semantic intelligence layer with governed write-back to operational systems of record, is the critical differentiator. Unlike Databricks/Snowflake (analytical read-path) or ServiceNow (workflow automation), Palantir built the operational write-path first and added the data platform underneath, creating structural advantages in complex, regulated, mission-critical environments. The central tension remains valuation, and the terms have now shifted twice: June 2026's worst month on record took the stock ~46% off the $207.52 ATH to a $106 52-week low, compressing the multiple to ~33x forward revenue at the trough (from ~52-79x trailing at authoring); the Q2 print then round-tripped the de-rate to $174.04 at the 2026-08-14 close (~$400B market cap), +64% off the low, 16% below ATH, and back to ~49x forward revenue on the raised $8.15B guide. Multiple mean-reversion ran both directions around a still-compounding business; of the three structural narratives priced in June (AI-agent / usage-based commoditization; European sovereignty rejections in France and the UK; a US defense CR freeze delaying federal revenue 6-9 months), the defense-freeze leg was contradicted by Q2's +90% U.S. government print while the other two remain live. The defense revenue floor (U.S. government $809M in Q2 alone, $3.2B+ annualized, +90% YoY, anchored by the $10B Army Enterprise Agreement and $1.275B Maven ceiling) still provides downside protection pure-play commercial AI companies lack. See [[Research/2026-06-29 - PLTR - June 2026 Sell-Off Through Mental Models - news]].

---

# [145/159] Theses/SIVE - Sivers Semiconductors.md
**thesis · ticker: SIVE · status: monitoring · conviction: low · sector: Optical Networking & Photonics · date: 2026-05-22 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

The only listed pure-play on the External Light Source (ELS) layer of the co-packaged optics ramp, trading at ~55x EV/Revenue after a 1,768% 3-month rally that priced in a transformation Sivers has not yet executed. The Photonics segment (Glasgow-based 100mm InP fab, "InP100" platform) supplies DFB lasers and laser arrays to Ayar Labs, Aeva, POET, O-Net + Enablence, Jabil, and Imec: virtually every Western CPO contender that lacks vertical InP capability and cannot secure Lumentum or Coherent allocation. The Wireless segment (Stockholm mmWave 5G transceivers) is dilutive but provides optionality on Tier-1 telecom OEM design wins and Tachyon Networks 28GHz FWA modules. Full-year 2025 revenue restated to SEK 306.6M (+25% YoY) under PCAOB upgrade ahead of Nasdaq NY dual-listing, against SEK 186.5M net loss and SEK 43.5M year-end cash, repaired temporarily by a SEK 125M directed share issue and a 10.85% coupon convertible bond whose conversion mechanics now sit deep in-the-money. The non-consensus thesis is structural overflow demand: as Lumentum sells out through 2027 and NVIDIA dual-allocates capacity, every second-tier SiPh/CPO contender (POET, Celestial AI via POET, Ayar Labs, hyperscaler internal programs) is forced to qualify a secondary InP supplier, and Sivers is the only listed Western option without 24-month qualification backlogs. The bear case is equally structural: 4-inch wafers cannot achieve the cost-per-chip of LITE's 6-inch Greensboro line (mid-2028) or Coherent's four 6-inch fabs (online ~2027), and the Swedish Economic Crime Authority's preliminary investigation into the Nasdaq listing announcement leaves the dual-listing, the primary catalyst the multiple expects, at material binary risk. Initial conviction **low**: this is a high-volatility small-cap option on the photonics overflow trade rather than a quality compounder.

→ **HIGH if**: (a) Photonics segment revenue crosses SEK 200M+ run-rate (i.e., SEK 50M+ quarterly Photonics revenue isolated in disclosure) by Q3 2026 reporting, AND (b) at least one hyperscaler-named CPO ELS qualification is announced (Microsoft, Meta, Google, Amazon, Oracle, or named NVIDIA platform reference), AND (c) Nasdaq NY dual-listing completes without probe escalation by YE 2026.

→ **LOW if**: (current state — see Log entry).

→ **CLOSE if**: any one of (a) Swedish Economic Crime Authority probe escalates to formal charges against named individuals OR (b) Sivers raises equity at >25% dilution within next 12 months OR (c) POET ELS partnership cancelled or materially restructured OR (d) Aeva (or whoever the LiDAR customer is) officially delays the announced ramp past 2029 OR (e) Photonics segment revenue declines YoY for two consecutive quarters during the 2026-2027 capacity-binding window when it should be growing fastest.

# [146/159] Theses/SKM - SK Telecom.md
**thesis · ticker: SKM · status: monitoring · conviction: medium · sector: Telecommunications Services · date: 2026-04-26 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

The market values SK Telecom as a damaged Korean wireless incumbent at ~5.2x core EV/EBITDA, a discount to Western telecoms (7-9x) and only a slight premium to depressed Korean peers (KT, LG U+ at ~4x), and grudgingly awards a ~$1B option value to its 0.3% Anthropic stake. The non-consensus mark: at Yuanta's mark of $2.4B (₩3.5T using Anthropic IV $800B-$1T from secondary markets and rejected VC offers), the Anthropic stake is **16-22% of SKM's $14.5B market cap**. With Anthropic targeting an Oct 2026-Q1 2027 IPO at consensus $643B-$1.04T, IFRS will force a mark-to-market step-up that adds ₩2-3T (~$1.5-2B) to SKT equity book value, a ~10-15% NAV step. Layer in: (a) FY26 operating recovery (Yuanta forecasts +71% YoY OP after the USIM breach + restructuring drag laps), (b) the May 29 2026 SK Broadband full absorption (cash squeeze-out, no SKT dilution) that dismantles the parent-sub holdco discount, (c) AIDC revenue growing 35% YoY with 1GW Ulsan capacity contracted into Stargate Korea (OpenAI MOU signed Oct 2025) and the Anthropic relationship, and (d) a ≥50% adjusted profit shareholder return policy that could support 7-8% total yield post-recovery. The skeptical IC view that this is "Korea discount + holdco trap + AI bubble option" misses three structural facts: SKT executed a clean chaebol unwind in 2021 (SK Square absorbed all non-telecom holdings), the breach impact is fading with H2 2025 churn already stabilized, and the Anthropic stake's mark-to-market sensitivity means **every $100B Anthropic re-rate moves SKT NAV by ~3% of market cap**, asymmetry that compresses sharply at IPO.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if**: Anthropic IPO prices ≥$700B AND SKT FY26 H1 OP runs ≥₩900B (Yuanta-implied half-year pace) AND Sovereign AI Phase 2 (June 2026) advances SKT to final 2.

→ **HIGH if**: SK Telecom announces ≥₩500B FY26 buyback authorization in Q3 2026 (post-SK Broadband absorption integration), demonstrating capital return discipline despite AI capex cycle.

→ **LOW if**: Anthropic raises Series H at sub-$300B (down round) OR Anthropic IPO formally delayed past 2027 by company announcement.

→ **LOW if**: SKT FY26 ARPU declines to <₩29,500 sustained (below the ₩30K psychological floor) AND MNO subscriber base shrinks year-over-year for 2 consecutive quarters.

→ **LOW if**: Sovereign AI Phase 2 (June 2026) eliminates SKT, leaving LG + Upstage as final 2.

→ **CLOSE if**: Anthropic IPO clears ≥$700B AND SKT trades to $50+ ADR (+30%) — thesis monetized, take profit and rotate to lower-multiple AI exposure.

→ **CLOSE if**: USIM breach tail liability discloses ≥₩500B additional charges (e.g., EU GDPR fine + KR class action settlement) AND FY26 OP guidance cut materially below Yuanta's ₩1.8T — combined deterioration of recovery thesis.

$1,641/share (up ~5,761% from $28 spin-off debut, still -30% below the July ATH of $2,354), FY2026 revenue $20.25B (+175% YoY) closing on a Q4 of $8.97B at 84.6% non-GAAP gross margin, Q1 FY2027 guided to $10.3–10.8B at 83–85%, and HBF (High-Bandwidth Flash) emerging as a potentially transformative new memory tier for AI inference. SanDisk re-listed on NASDAQ in February 2025 after spinning off from Western Digital and accesses NAND wafers at cost-plus through the Flash Ventures JV with Kioxia, giving it integrated-like economics without full fab ownership. Three questions define the investment case: whether HBF creates a multi-billion-dollar market that doesn't yet exist, whether NAND structural tailwinds sustain through 2027, and whether the margin trajectory is structural (business model transformation) or cyclical (pricing euphoria).

# [148/159] Theses/SOI - Soitec.md
**thesis · ticker: SOI · status: draft · conviction: medium · sector: Semiconductor Capital Equipment · date: 2026-06-08 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

Soitec is two businesses traded as one engineered-substrate company, with sell-side anchored to the wrong half. RF-SOI / Mobile Communications (52% of FY26 revenue, -41% YoY constant currency, Q4 -52% YoY) dominates the framing and drove the FY26 revenue collapse to €592M (-34%), but the underlying story is that Photonics-SOI (>$100M FY26, mgmt guides 20-30% annual growth) is the **sole volume-qualified substrate supplier at all three CPO silicon-photonics foundries** (TSMC COUPE, GlobalFoundries Fotonix, Tower PH18) at the exact moment hyperscale CPO commercialisation begins (TSMC COUPE in risk production with AMD Feb 2026; Broadcom Bailly has shipped 50K+ switches; NVIDIA Quantum-X engines are TSMC COUPE-based). The non-consensus claim is that the market is valuing this near-monopoly substrate position at zero while pricing in a worst-case RF-SOI extension, the inverse of the actual structural setup. Conviction is medium: the Photonics-SOI thesis is real and the moat (qualification across three foundries + 4,300+ Smart Cut patents + 30-year process refinement) is durable; offset by a ~€6.7B market cap at ~11.5x EV/Revenue / ~45x EV/EBITDA on FY26 leaves no margin for the cyclical earnings miss that low FY27 fab loading + FX + lower French CIR funding has already been pre-announced for, and the CEO transition (Barnabé out 31 March 2026, Rémont in 1 April 2026) is a 6-12 month strategy-clarity gap with the formal AGM director vote not until July 2026.

## Conviction Triggers

**→ HIGH if**: Q1'27 (Jul-Aug 2026) discloses Photonics-SOI growth >35% YoY (vs 20-30% guide) AND RF-SOI Mobile YoY decline narrows to <-20% (vs -41% FY26) AND Rémont AGM (July 2026) anchors new medium-term Photonics-SOI mix-shift framework. Three-condition gate validates Photonics-SOI ramp velocity, cyclical bottom passing, and strategic continuity.

**→ HIGH if**: Soitec announces formal multi-year Photonics-SOI capacity commitment with a named hyperscale customer (NVDA, AVGO, GOOGL, MSFT, AMZN) ≥$50M; would force sell-side to price the monopoly explicitly.

**→ LOW if**: Q1'27 reveals Photonics-SOI growth <15% YoY (below the mgmt guide floor) AND RF-SOI Mobile continues at -40% or worse; signals the bull-case timeline is broken on both legs simultaneously.

**→ LOW if**: Shin-Etsu publicly qualifies photonics-grade SOI at GlobalFoundries or Tower Semiconductor: breaks the monopoly narrative; would compress the Photonics-SOI valuation premium even ahead of any volume impact.

**→ CLOSE if**: STMicroelectronics announces SmartSiC qualification delay >12 months OR ST commercially deprioritises SmartSiC in favour of bulk-SiC alternatives; combined with Photonics-SOI growth missing the 20-30% guide for 2 consecutive quarters, would invalidate the diversification thesis at the segment level.

**→ CLOSE if**: Market cap reaches €10B+ (~€215+/share) without commensurate revenue / EBITDA ramp; pure multiple expansion on optionality becomes uninvestable for a substrate company with a one-year revenue cycle visible.

## Conviction Triggers

- **→ CLOSE if:** capital-recovery clock fails — disclosed D&A lives exceed demonstrated CSA duration with no long-duration replacement — while Connectivity margin is simultaneously degrading (no residual to fall back on)

# [153/159] Theses/TSEM - Tower Semiconductor.md
**thesis · ticker: TSEM · status: draft · conviction: low · sector: Semiconductor Foundries · date: 2026-07-14 · metrics as of: 2026-07-14**

## Summary

The operating reclassification is real; the stock already prices more than management's plan. Tower is moving from a 23% gross-margin specialty foundry into a qualification-gated optical platform: silicon-photonics revenue was about $230M in 2025, while its largest customers have contractually committed $1.3B of wafers delivered in 2027, backed by $290M of prepayments, and management models the incremental revenue at 59% gross margin. The equity value of about $25.5B at $229.68 on 2026-07-14 equals roughly 34x management's 2028 net-profit target of $750M and 9.1x its $2.8B revenue target. A 15% annual return through year-end 2028 requires about $36B of equity value: 48x target earnings, or more than $1.0B of net income at a 35x exit multiple. Initial conviction is low: the business inflection has unusually hard contractual evidence, but the entry price requires a material target beat while Tower still faces a fivefold capacity ramp, opaque anchor-customer concentration, GlobalFoundries and TSMC competition, and a live USITC patent case.

## Conviction Triggers

→ HIGH if: Tower lifts credible 2028 net income to at least $1.0B, exits 2026 above 32% gross margin, converts 2027 SiPho deliveries at or above $1.3B without material refunds, and the equity trades at no more than 25x that updated earnings base.

→ LOW if: Revenue and SiPho capacity track guidance but the 2028 net-profit target remains below $900M while equity value stays above $25B, leaving execution upside already discounted.

→ CLOSE if: Contracted 2027 SiPho deliveries are revised below $1.0B, two consecutive quarters show SiPho shipment or yield misses, customer advances are materially refunded, or a USITC remedy threatens US importation of a major product family.

# [154/159] Theses/TSM - Taiwan Semiconductor.md
**thesis · ticker: TSM · status: active · conviction: high · sector: Semiconductor Foundries · date: 2026-04-19 · metrics as of: 2026-08-15**

## Summary

TSMC is a functional monopoly on leading-edge compute, and the market has now largely paid for it, which moves the question from recognition to durability. 92% share of advanced nodes (≤7nm), a record 67.7% gross margin in Q2 2026 (+910bps YoY), and a sold-out N2 at a 10–20% premium confirm monopoly rents are being harvested rather than still arriving. The non-consensus angle remains CoWoS and COUPE as separable revenue lines (advanced-packaging + photonics TAM expansion) plus A16 backside-power-rail exclusivity to NVDA Feynman (2027) that deepens customer lock-in beyond the wafer contract. High conviction (since 2026-05-22) with a fully-priced caveat: the 2028 bull-case earnings (~$16–17 EPS) are landing in 2026 (~$17.5/ADR FY26E derived), and the July 2026 beat-and-raise (FY26 growth >40%, capex $60–64B, US commitment $265B) raised the fundamental ceiling yet the price has gone nowhere since: ~$426 close 2026-08-14 (~24x FY26E, ~$2.2T mcap, −11% off the $479 high) after a $374.67 trough into hyperscaler-print week, with big-4 2026 capex guides (~$655-685B aggregate) and July revenue +44.7% YoY raising estimates against the flat price, so the remaining edge is durability-beyond-2028 (packaging annuity, photonics, A14 cadence) plus the market's new margin-peak fear being dilution arithmetic rather than pricing erosion. Taiwan tail risk is real, NVDA (~22%) + AAPL (~18%) ≈ 40% concentration is now AI-capex-cyclical, and the $265B US buildout dilutes margins 2–3pts early-stage widening to 3–4pts by guidance. The investment case is durability-of-monopoly over 5 years, not the next print.

## Conviction Triggers

### Original framework (2026-04-19) — HIGH block EXHAUSTED by the Q2'26 print; retained for audit

→ **HIGH if**: *(all three legs fired: GM 67.7% Q2'26 ✓ · capex $60-64B ✓ · Feynman sole A16 customer ✓)*
- 2026 full-year gross margin reports >60% AND
- Q2 or Q3 2026 capex guide is raised to $45B+ AND
- NVDA Feynman confirmed exclusively on A16 with volume commit through 2028
- Rationale: confirms pricing power expanding (Insight #5), scale commitment locked (Insight #3), monopoly durability through 2028

### Re-armed 2026-07-24 (post-Q2'26 print — [[Research/2026-07-24 - TSM Q2 2026 Results - earnings]])

→ **HIGH-reaffirm if**:
- FY2026 closes at >40% USD revenue growth AND
- Q3 2026 GM lands ≥66% (upper guide half: N2 dilution absorbed by pricing) AND
- First 2027 capex guide (Jan 2027) ≥$70B
- Rationale: monopoly rents absorbing dilution + demand outrunning the buildout, the durability-beyond-2028 leg strengthening

→ **LOW if**:
- Any 2027 quarter HPC revenue growth <10% YoY (AI capex cycle topping) OR
- GM <63% for 2 consecutive quarters while announced price hikes continue (dilution structurally worse than guided; pricing failing to absorb) OR
- Arizona N2 ramp delayed past Q3 2028 OR Intel 18A lands 2+ major external customers (carried over, unfired) OR
- A named production-scale CoWoS-alternative win (e.g., Google TPU v9 volume on Intel EMIB-T) OR confirmed Nova Lake-scale N2 order pull-back to Intel 18A
- Rationale: invalidates monopoly rent extension OR breaks the "no second" thesis at the wafer or packaging layer

→ **CLOSE if** (unchanged from original):
- Taiwan blockade/invasion or equivalent catastrophic geopolitical event OR
- NVDA+AAPL combined revenue concentration exceeds 50% (systemic risk unmanageable) OR
- Gross margin falls below 52% for 2 consecutive quarters with Arizona mix still <20% (structural cost increase, not transition)

# [157/159] Theses/VICR - Vicor Corporation.md
**thesis · ticker: VICR · status: active · conviction: medium · sector: Data Center Power & Cooling · date: 2026-04-28 · metrics as of: 2026-08-16**

## Summary

Vicor is a 45-year-old founder-controlled power module specialist whose Vertical Power Delivery (VPD) architecture is structurally required to feed the 2,000A+ currents of NVIDIA's Rubin/Rubin Ultra GPUs (H2 2026 / H2 2027), the same company the market wrote off in 2023 after Monolithic Power Systems took the H100 socket. Q1 2026 already shows the inflection: revenue +20% YoY, gross margin 55.2% (industrial-power norm is 30-40%), backlog +70%, book-to-bill >2x, and a high-margin patent-licensing revenue stream that ITC enforcement (Limited Exclusion Order against Delta/Cyntec/Foxconn) has established at a ~$60M annualised run-rate (Q1 2026 royalties ~$15M), though management paused new licensing agreements until the second ITC case's final determination in 2027, so the FY26 $570M guide assumes no incremental licensing (the ~$300M figure is the order backlog, not the licensing pipeline). Two non-consensus angles drive this: (1) Rubin's current density is past the threshold where lateral power delivery (MPS's product) physically works, forcing the AI ecosystem back to VPD via the side door, Vicor's home turf, independent of whether Vicor wins the GPU socket itself; (2) the LEO converts share losses into licensing royalties, so a competitor winning a socket while infringing Vicor patents is a mixed positive, not a pure negative. Stock is +400% TTM but −36% off the July $366 peak and trades ~$10.6B / $234.74 at ~69–82x the thesis FY26E EPS band (~68x Street $3.44). July's $13.1B prepaid more of the structural rerate than spot does; 68x on a $570M-revenue guide is still a $2.5B-model multiple, not an industrial. Conviction medium because the founder is 78, the FY26 guide has a "contingent on licensing agreements awaiting resolution in 2027" footnote, and the Andover capacity ramp ($1B → $1.5B/yr Fab One) is execution-gated.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if**: Vera Rubin NVL144 production confirms Vicor VPD content per processor (via NVIDIA reference design partner disclosure or 8-K supply agreement) AND FY27 licensing guide is raised above $300M cumulative (vs $300M pipeline today) AND Q4 2026 product-only GM holds above 50%. The combination confirms architectural moat + structural rents + durable margin profile.

→ **LOW if**: Federal Circuit narrows LEO scope materially in 2027 appeal decision (compressing licensing pipeline by >40%) OR NVIDIA Rubin Ultra reference design lists Flex/MPS/TI as primary VPD vendor with Vicor as secondary source OR two consecutive quarters show Advanced Products growth below 10% YoY (suggesting AI module mix is rolling over). Any of these signals a structural break in either the IP moat or the architectural-necessity thesis.

→ **CLOSE if**: Founder Patrizio Vinciarelli departs without a publicly-announced and equity-aligned successor (operational void + concentration unwind triggers structural revaluation) OR Federal Circuit invalidates core VPD patents at PTAB (eliminating licensing revenue stream) OR Monolithic Power Systems announces and ships a clean-sheet vertical PDN product matching Vicor's PDN-density advantage at lower cost (the architectural moat is buyable, not built: Vicor's lead collapses).

# [158/159] Theses/VRT - Vertiv Holdings.md
**thesis · ticker: VRT · status: monitoring · conviction: medium · sector: Data Center Power & Cooling · date: 2026-04-21 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

Vertiv is the dominant pure-play on AI-era data center critical infrastructure (power, thermal, IT management), carrying a $15B backlog at 2.9x book-to-bill and guiding 27-29% organic growth in 2026 on 22-23% operating margin. The market already recognizes the AI tailwind: 22 of 26 analysts rate Buy, the stock is up 336% in 12 months, and trades ~43x 2026 EPS. Consensus is underwriting capacity/backlog, not the physics-derived moat. At >100kW/rack, liquid cooling becomes a thermodynamic and co-design problem; Vertiv's NVIDIA/Intel integration loop and OCP committee authorship (with Meta) create a standards-insider position that generalist competitors (Schneider, Eaton) cannot replicate by bolt-on M&A. The asymmetry is cycle timing vs. durability: bulls and bears agree the AI capex cycle will peak; disagreement is when, and whether Vertiv's moat survives the reset. Medium conviction reflects high thesis quality priced against near-cycle-peak sentiment.

## Conviction Triggers

→ **HIGH if**: Q2 2026 organic orders grow >50% YoY AND Q2 liquid cooling share metric (disclosed or inferred from commentary) holds above 35% AND hyperscaler capex guides for FY2027 raise by 10%+ vs. FY2026 run-rate. This combination confirms demand durability + moat durability + cycle extension.

→ **LOW if**: Two consecutive quarters (any Q2-Q4 2026) show organic orders negative YoY OR operating margin drops below 21% with explicit "customer mix" commentary OR hyperscaler (Meta/Google/Microsoft) announces proprietary liquid cooling reference architecture in competition with Vertiv 360AI. Any of these signals a structural break in the thesis.

→ **CLOSE if**: NVIDIA acquires a liquid cooling company OR announces DGX-integrated thermal management for post-Blackwell systems, eliminating the co-design moat. Alternative trigger: AI capex guides from top-3 hyperscalers collectively cut by >15% for FY2027 relative to FY2026 (indicating a genuine cycle peak, not a pause). Stock would likely be down 40%+ before these trigger cleanly, but the analytical case breaks regardless of price action.

# [159/159] Theses/WTC - WiseTech Global.md
**thesis · ticker: WTC · status: monitoring · conviction: high · sector: Logistics & Supply Chain Software · date: 2026-04-15 · metrics as of: 2026-07-12**

## Summary

CargoWise: 24 of 25 top freight forwarders, 43 of 50 top 3PLs, 18,000+ organizations across 170+ countries, processing ~75% of global customs transaction data: the closest thing to a natural monopoly in enterprise software. The $2.1B E2open acquisition (closed August 2025) extends the monopoly upstream into shipper-side planning, adding 500,000+ trading partners. H1 FY2026: integration 18 months ahead of schedule, US$50M annualized synergies achieved, E2open at A$249.4M revenue and 34% EBITDA margins (+6pp vs. pro forma). The market is pricing the governance crisis, not the business: founder Richard White's October 2024 ouster, February 2025 forced return (four directors resigned), and October 2025 ASIC/AFP insider trading raid have driven the stock from ~A$100 to ~A$37, trading at ~7.5x EV/Revenue vs. Descartes at ~15x on a smaller, less dominant asset. The market has conflated a governance discount (resolvable, non-operational) with business impairment (not occurring): a rare opportunity to acquire a monopoly at cyclical-low valuation while the business accelerates through AI automation and E2open integration.
